> A 1 [M] Bird token is a domainless unit token with 1 Might, the Bird tag, and the Deflect keyword. See rule 809. Deflect for more information.

### [official-rule] 184.8 - A Brush battlefield token is a domainless battlefield token with “Bird, Cat, Dog, Poro,...

- parent_rule: 184
- section_id: 184
- page: 20
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Brush battlefield token is a domainless battlefield token with “Bird, Cat, Dog, Poro, and Ivern units here have +1 [M]” and “When you score here, you may replace this with the battlefield it replaced.”

### [official-rule] 184.9 - The Baron Pit battlefield token is a domainless battlefield token with “Units can move...

- parent_rule: 184
- section_id: 184
- page: 20
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The Baron Pit battlefield token is a domainless battlefield token with “Units can move here from anywhere.”

### [official-rule] 185 - Control

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 185
- page: 20
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Control

### [official-rule] 186 - Control is the concept of a player having influence of a Game Object and applies differ...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 186
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Control is the concept of a player having influence of a Game Object and applies differently to different card types.

### [official-rule] 187 - Battlefields

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 187
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Battlefields

### [official-rule] 187.1 - Battlefields

- parent_rule: 187
- section_id: 187
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Control is established over Battlefields through the course of play.

### [official-rule] 187.2 - Battlefields

- parent_rule: 187
- section_id: 187
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Control is a binary state for Battlefields and an Identifier for players.

### [official-rule] 187.2.a - Battlefields

- parent_rule: 187.2
- section_id: 187
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Battlefield is Controlled or Uncontrolled.

### [official-rule] 187.2.b - Battlefields

- parent_rule: 187.2
- section_id: 187
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Battlefield is Controlled by a specific player or Controlled by no one.

### [official-rule] 187.3 - Battlefields

- parent_rule: 187
- section_id: 187
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Control can be Contested through the course of play.

### [official-rule] 187.3.a - Battlefields

- parent_rule: 187.3
- section_id: 187
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Contested is a temporary status applied to the battlefield when a Unit controlled by a Player who does not currently Control that Battlefield Moves or otherwise becomes present there.

### [official-rule] 187.3.a.1 - Battlefields

- parent_rule: 187.3.a
- section_id: 187
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Units moving to or being played to a battlefield apply Contested status if that battlefield is not already contested and that Unit’s controller does not already control that battlefield.

### [official-rule] 187.3.b - Battlefields

- parent_rule: 187.3
- section_id: 187
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Battlefield remains Contested until Control is established or re-established.

### [official-rule] 187.3.c - Battlefields

- parent_rule: 187.3
- section_id: 187
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The state of a Battlefield being Contested is used to determine when Combat should occur, when a Non-Combat Showdown should occur, and when Control will change.

### [official-rule] 187.3.d - Battlefields

- parent_rule: 187.3
- section_id: 187
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> At this time Game Effects cannot reference this status.

### [official-rule] 187.4 - Battlefields

- parent_rule: 187
- section_id: 187
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Control is established by having Units at a Battlefield at the end of a Showdown or Combat after applying the contested status.

### [official-rule] 187.4.a - Battlefields

- parent_rule: 187.4
- section_id: 187
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If a player controls Units at a Battlefield, outside of Combat, they maintain Control of that Battlefield for as long as they have Units at that Battlefield.

### [official-rule] 187.4.b - Battlefields

- parent_rule: 187.4
- section_id: 187
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> While a Combat or Showdown is ongoing at a Battlefield, Control of that Battlefield cannot change until instructed by steps of the Combat or Showdown.

### [official-rule] 187.4.c - Battlefields

- parent_rule: 187.4
- section_id: 187
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If a player has no Units at a Battlefield and the turn is in an Open state, they lose Control of that Battlefield in the following cleanup, unless there is a Combat or Showdown ongoing there.

### [official-rule] 187.5 - Battlefields

- parent_rule: 187
- section_id: 187
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Control is a constant state.

### [official-rule] 187.6 - Battlefields

- parent_rule: 187
- section_id: 187
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Control of a Battlefield determines Control of its Abilities.

### [official-rule] 187.6.a - Battlefields

- parent_rule: 187.6
- section_id: 187
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> While a Battlefield is Controlled, its Controller controls its Abilities. That player takes responsibility for adding them to the Chain if applicable, and makes all choices required by them unless otherwise specified.

### [official-rule] 187.6.b - Battlefields

- parent_rule: 187.6
- section_id: 187
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> While a Battlefield is Uncontrolled, its Abilities are also Uncontrolled. The Turn Player takes responsibility for adding them to the Chain if applicable, makes all choices required by them unless otherwise specified, and is treated as their Controller if any game rule or effect requires one. Example: The Arena’s Greatest is a battlefield that reads “At the start of each player's first Beginning Phase, that player gains 1 point.” This ability will usually trigger while the battlefield has no controller. If it does, the Turn Player goes through the steps of adding the ability to the chain and receives priority after doing so, exactly as if they controlled the ability.

### [official-rule] 187.6.c - Battlefields

- parent_rule: 187.6
- section_id: 187
- page: 21
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> “You” in a battlefield’s abilities refers to the battlefield’s Controller, as does the implied “you” in instructions like “draw 1.” If the battlefield has no Controller, “you” refers to no one, and all such instructions are ignored.

### [official-rule] 188 - Everything Else

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 188
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Everything Else

### [official-rule] 188.1 - Everything Else

- parent_rule: 188
- section_id: 188
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When a player Plays a Card or other Game Object, they are established as that Game Object's Controller.

### [official-rule] 188.2 - Everything Else

- parent_rule: 188
- section_id: 188
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> For Spells, they are the Spell's Controller.

### [official-rule] 188.2.a - Everything Else

- parent_rule: 188.2
- section_id: 188
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> That player chooses targets.

### [official-rule] 188.2.b - Everything Else

- parent_rule: 188.2
- section_id: 188
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> That player chooses modes.

### [official-rule] 188.2.c - Everything Else

- parent_rule: 188.2
- section_id: 188
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> That player pays costs.

### [official-rule] 188.3 - Everything Else

- parent_rule: 188
- section_id: 188
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> For Permanents and Runes, when they Enter the Board, that player is assigned as that Game Object's Controller.

### [official-rule] 188.3.a - Everything Else

- parent_rule: 188.3
- section_id: 188
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> That player may make decisions about the Game Object's Inherent Abilities.

### [official-rule] 188.3.b - Everything Else

- parent_rule: 188.3
- section_id: 188
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> That player may make decisions about the Game Object's Unique Abilities.

### [official-rule] 188.3.c - Everything Else

- parent_rule: 188.3
- section_id: 188
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> That player may make decisions about any game effects or decisions necessary while the card is being played.

### [official-rule] 188.3.d - Everything Else

- parent_rule: 188.3
- section_id: 188
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> That player may make decisions about any game effects created from "When you play me" effects of Permanents.

### [official-rule] 188.4 - Everything Else

- parent_rule: 188
- section_id: 188
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> For Abilities, they are the Ability’s Controller.

### [official-rule] 188.4.a - Everything Else

- parent_rule: 188.4
- section_id: 188
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> By default, the Controller of an Ability’s Source is the Controller of that Ability.

### [official-rule] 188.4.b - Everything Else

- parent_rule: 188.4
- section_id: 188
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Changes to Control of an Ability’s Source do not change Control of that Ability.

### [official-rule] 188.4.c - Everything Else

- parent_rule: 188.4
- section_id: 188
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> That player chooses targets.

### [official-rule] 188.4.d - Everything Else

- parent_rule: 188.4
- section_id: 188
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> That player chooses modes.

### [official-rule] 188.4.e - Everything Else

- parent_rule: 188.4
- section_id: 188
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> That player pays costs.

### [official-rule] 189 - When a game effect or rules text refers to the Controller of a specific object, it can...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 189
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When a game effect or rules text refers to the Controller of a specific object, it can be referring to either context interchangeably.

### [official-rule] 189.1 - The method of assignment of control is different, but the status of Control is the same...

- parent_rule: 189
- section_id: 189
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The method of assignment of control is different, but the status of Control is the same across all Game Objects.

### [official-rule] 300 - Playing the Game

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 300
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Playing the Game

### [official-rule] 301 - The Turn

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 301
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The Turn

### [official-rule] 302 - Play continues cyclically until one player wins.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 302
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Play continues cyclically until one player wins.

### [official-rule] 303 - The phases of a turn are rigid, but the actions taken during those steps can be done in...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 303
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The phases of a turn are rigid, but the actions taken during those steps can be done in any order, unless otherwise specified.

### [official-rule] 303.1 - Game Actions of any nature are performed one at a time and are executed completely.

- parent_rule: 303
- section_id: 303
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Game Actions of any nature are performed one at a time and are executed completely.

### [official-rule] 303.2 - Game Actions cannot be performed simultaneously for any reason.

- parent_rule: 303
- section_id: 303
- page: 22
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Game Actions cannot be performed simultaneously for any reason.

### [official-rule] 303.2.a - If one or more game actions, game effects, or Triggered Abilities are activated simulta...

- parent_rule: 303.2
- section_id: 303
- page: 22-23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If one or more game actions, game effects, or Triggered Abilities are activated simultaneously, then Turn Order is referenced to organize the sequence of actions. See rule 382. Triggered Abilities for more information.

### [official-rule] 304 - The Turn Player is the player taking the current turn.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 304
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The Turn Player is the player taking the current turn.

### [official-rule] 305 - When there are no items on the Chain and the Turn Player cannot or chooses not to perfo...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 305
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When there are no items on the Chain and the Turn Player cannot or chooses not to perform any Discretionary Actions, the current phase or step of the turn ends and the next phase, step, or turn begins.

### [official-rule] 306 - The Turn Player changes when the current Turn Player reaches the End of all of the Phas...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 306
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The Turn Player changes when the current Turn Player reaches the End of all of the Phases of their Turn.

### [official-rule] 307 - States of the Turn

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 307
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> States of the Turn

### [official-rule] 308 - At any given time, the turn is in either a Neutral State or a Showdown State.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 308
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> At any given time, the turn is in either a Neutral State or a Showdown State.

### [official-rule] 308.1 - If a Showdown or Combat is in progress, the turn is in a Showdown State.

- parent_rule: 308
- section_id: 308
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If a Showdown or Combat is in progress, the turn is in a Showdown State.

### [official-rule] 308.1.a - Only cards and abilities with the Action or Reaction keywords can be played or activate...

- parent_rule: 308.1
- section_id: 308
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Only cards and abilities with the Action or Reaction keywords can be played or activated in a Showdown State.

### [official-rule] 308.2 - If no Showdown or Combat is in progress, the turn is in a Neutral State.

- parent_rule: 308
- section_id: 308
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If no Showdown or Combat is in progress, the turn is in a Neutral State.

### [official-rule] 309 - At any given time, the turn is in either an Open State or a Closed State.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 309
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> At any given time, the turn is in either an Open State or a Closed State.

### [official-rule] 309.1 - If a Chain exists, the turn is in a Closed State.

- parent_rule: 309
- section_id: 309
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If a Chain exists, the turn is in a Closed State.

### [official-rule] 309.1.a - Only cards and abilities with the Reaction keyword can be played or activated in a Clos...

- parent_rule: 309.1
- section_id: 309
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Only cards and abilities with the Reaction keyword can be played or activated in a Closed State.

### [official-rule] 309.2 - If no Chain exists, the turn is in an Open State.

- parent_rule: 309
- section_id: 309
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If no Chain exists, the turn is in an Open State.

### [official-rule] 310 - These descriptions can be combined, such that the turn is always in one of these four s...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 310
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> These descriptions can be combined, such that the turn is always in one of these four states:

### [official-rule] 310.1 - Neutral Open: There is no Showdown or Combat in progress and no Chain exists.

- parent_rule: 310
- section_id: 310
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Neutral Open: There is no Showdown or Combat in progress and no Chain exists.

### [official-rule] 310.1.a - By default, cards can be played and abilities activated only when a player has priority...

- parent_rule: 310.1
- section_id: 310
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> By default, cards can be played and abilities activated only when a player has priority on their turn in a Neutral Open state.

### [official-rule] 310.2 - Neutral Closed: There is no Showdown or Combat in progress and a Chain exists.

- parent_rule: 310
- section_id: 310
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Neutral Closed: There is no Showdown or Combat in progress and a Chain exists.

### [official-rule] 310.3 - Showdown Open: A Showdown or Combat is in progress and no Chain exists.

- parent_rule: 310
- section_id: 310
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Showdown Open: A Showdown or Combat is in progress and no Chain exists.

### [official-rule] 310.4 - Showdown Closed: A Showdown or Combat is in progress and a Chain exists.

- parent_rule: 310
- section_id: 310
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Showdown Closed: A Showdown or Combat is in progress and a Chain exists.

### [official-rule] 311 - Priority and Focus

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 311
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Priority and Focus

### [official-rule] 312 - At any given time, up to one player has Priority.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 312
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> At any given time, up to one player has Priority.

### [official-rule] 312.1 - Priority is the singular exclusive right to take Discretionary Actions. See rule 410.1....

- parent_rule: 312
- section_id: 312
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Priority is the singular exclusive right to take Discretionary Actions. See rule 410.1. Discretionary Actions for more information.

### [official-rule] 312.1.a - The player with Priority can take appropriately timed Discretionary Actions.

- parent_rule: 312.1
- section_id: 312
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The player with Priority can take appropriately timed Discretionary Actions.

### [official-rule] 312.1.b - If no player has Priority, no player can take Discretionary Actions.

- parent_rule: 312.1
- section_id: 312
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If no player has Priority, no player can take Discretionary Actions.

### [official-rule] 312.1.b.1 - Players can always take and make choices for Limited Actions when instructed, regardles...

- parent_rule: 312.1.b
- section_id: 312
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Players can always take and make choices for Limited Actions when instructed, regardless of Priority.

### [official-rule] 312.2 - A player receives Priority at the following times:

- parent_rule: 312
- section_id: 312
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A player receives Priority at the following times:

### [official-rule] 312.2.a - When the turn is in a Neutral Open State during their Main Phase.

- parent_rule: 312.2
- section_id: 312
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When the turn is in a Neutral Open State during their Main Phase.

### [official-rule] 312.2.b - When the turn is in a Showdown State and they gain Focus.

- parent_rule: 312.2
- section_id: 312
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When the turn is in a Showdown State and they gain Focus.

### [official-rule] 312.2.c - When the turn is in a Closed State and they control the next item on the Chain.

- parent_rule: 312.2
- section_id: 312
- page: 23
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When the turn is in a Closed State and they control the next item on the Chain.

### [official-rule] 312.2.d - When the turn is in a Closed State, they are the next Player in Turn Order, and the pla...

- parent_rule: 312.2
- section_id: 312
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When the turn is in a Closed State, they are the next Player in Turn Order, and the player with Priority passes.

### [official-rule] 312.3 - When a player is granted Priority, it is either created if no player has it or taken fr...

- parent_rule: 312
- section_id: 312
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When a player is granted Priority, it is either created if no player has it or taken from the player with Priority.

### [official-rule] 313 - At any given time, up to one player has Focus.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 313
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> At any given time, up to one player has Focus.

### [official-rule] 313.1 - Focus is the permission to take appropriately timed Discretionary Actions when the turn...

- parent_rule: 313
- section_id: 313
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Focus is the permission to take appropriately timed Discretionary Actions when the turn is in a Showdown Open State. See rule 307. States of the Turn for more information.

### [official-rule] 313.1.a - The player with Focus must obey any additional restrictions on which Discretionary Acti...

- parent_rule: 313.1
- section_id: 313
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The player with Focus must obey any additional restrictions on which Discretionary Actions may be performed. Example: A player with Focus may not play spells or activate abilities that don't have the Action or Reaction keywords.

### [official-rule] 313.2 - A player who gains Focus also gains Priority.

- parent_rule: 313
- section_id: 313
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A player who gains Focus also gains Priority.

### [official-rule] 313.3 - A player who passes Priority retains Focus.

- parent_rule: 313
- section_id: 313
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A player who passes Priority retains Focus.

### [official-rule] 313.4 - A player may not make discretionary actions with Focus unless they also possess Priority.

- parent_rule: 313
- section_id: 313
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A player may not make discretionary actions with Focus unless they also possess Priority.

### [official-rule] 313.5 - If the turn is in a Neutral State, no player has Focus.

- parent_rule: 313
- section_id: 313
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the turn is in a Neutral State, no player has Focus.

### [official-rule] 314 - Phases of the Turn

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 314
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Phases of the Turn

### [official-rule] 315 - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 315
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Start of Turn

### [official-rule] 315.1 - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315
- section_id: 315
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Awaken Phase

### [official-rule] 315.1.a - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315.1
- section_id: 315
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The following Task becomes Outstanding:

### [official-rule] 315.1.b - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315.1
- section_id: 315
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 1. The Turn Player readies all Game Objects they control that are able to be readied. See rule 415. Ready for more information.

### [official-rule] 315.2 - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315
- section_id: 315
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Beginning Phase

### [official-rule] 315.2.a - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315.2
- section_id: 315
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Beginning Step

### [official-rule] 315.2.a.1 - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315.2.a
- section_id: 315
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> At the start of Beginning Phase game effects take place.

### [official-rule] 315.2.b - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315.2
- section_id: 315
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Scoring Step

### [official-rule] 315.2.b.1 - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315.2.b
- section_id: 315
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The following Task becomes Outstanding:

### [official-rule] 315.2.b.2 - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315.2.b
- section_id: 315
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 1. The Turn Player Holds all Battlefields they Control. See rule 462. Scoring for more information.

### [official-rule] 315.2.b.3 - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315.2.b
- section_id: 315
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Reminder: In Modes of Play with Teams, Battlefields held by a Teammate of the Turn Player during this phase are disqualified from being scored this turn by the Turn Player.

### [official-rule] 315.3 - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315
- section_id: 315
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Channel Phase

### [official-rule] 315.3.a - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315.3
- section_id: 315
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The following Task becomes Outstanding:

### [official-rule] 315.3.b - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315.3
- section_id: 315
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 1. The Turn Player channels 2 runes from their Rune Deck. See rule 430. Channel for more information.

### [official-rule] 315.3.b.1 - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315.3.b
- section_id: 315
- page: 24
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If there are fewer than 2 runes in the Rune Deck, they channel as many as possible.

### [official-rule] 315.4 - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315
- section_id: 315
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Draw Phase

### [official-rule] 315.4.a - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315.4
- section_id: 315
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The following Task becomes Outstanding:

### [official-rule] 315.4.b - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315.4
- section_id: 315
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 1. The Turn Player draws 1.

### [official-rule] 315.4.b.1 - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315.4.b
- section_id: 315
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If there are no cards remaining in their Main Deck to draw, the Turn Player has been Burned Out. See rule 431. Burn Out for more information.

### [official-rule] 315.4.b.2 - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315.4.b
- section_id: 315
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> After completing the Burn Out the Turn Player still Draws 1.

### [official-rule] 315.4.c - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315.4
- section_id: 315
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The following Task becomes Outstanding:

### [official-rule] 315.4.d - Start of Turn

- parent_rule: 315.4
- section_id: 315
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 1. Each player's Rune Pool empties. Any unspent Energy and Power are lost. See rule 164. Rune Pools for more information.

### [official-rule] 316 - Main Phase

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 316
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Main Phase

### [official-rule] 316.1 - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316
- section_id: 316
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When all steps of the Start of Turn have been completed, the Main Phase begins.

### [official-rule] 316.2 - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316
- section_id: 316
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The Main Phase has no defined structure.

### [official-rule] 316.2.a - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316.2
- section_id: 316
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A player may take any number of Discretionary Actions they are able to perform during this phase. See rule 410.1. Discretionary Actions for more information.

### [official-rule] 316.2.b - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316.2
- section_id: 316
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> This is denoted as a Neutral Open State, and only the Turn Player has the ability to play spells or activate abilities. See rule 307. States of the Turn for more information.

### [official-rule] 316.2.b.1 - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316.2.b
- section_id: 316
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> In Modes of Play with teammates, the Turn Player's teammates may play spells and activate abilities, including ones without Action or Reaction. They can only do so when the Turn Player invites them to do so with their own Priority.

### [official-rule] 316.3 - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316
- section_id: 316
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> As a result of a player taking Discretionary Actions, one or more structured phases may occur.

### [official-rule] 316.4 - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316
- section_id: 316
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Combat

### [official-rule] 316.4.a - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316.4
- section_id: 316
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Combat occurs as a result of Units controlled by opposing players being present at the same Battlefield.

### [official-rule] 316.4.b - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316.4
- section_id: 316
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> This could be the result of a Standard Move Standard Action, a Spell, or other Game Effect.

### [official-rule] 316.4.c - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316.4
- section_id: 316
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The source effect does not change the structure or flow of Combat once initiated.

### [official-rule] 316.4.d - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316.4
- section_id: 316
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Combat can only occur between two players. See rule 454. Combat for more information.

### [official-rule] 316.4.e - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316.4
- section_id: 316
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Play proceeds following the steps of combat. See rule 458. The Steps of Combat for more information.

### [official-rule] 316.4.f - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316.4
- section_id: 316
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Combat will also include a Showdown.

### [official-rule] 316.5 - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316
- section_id: 316
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Showdowns

### [official-rule] 316.5.a - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316.5
- section_id: 316
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Showdown occurs when a Combat occurs.

### [official-rule] 316.5.a.1 - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316.5.a
- section_id: 316
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Showdowns that occur as a result of Combat are a Sub-Phase of Combat.

### [official-rule] 316.5.b - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316.5
- section_id: 316
- page: 25
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Showdown is marked as Staged at a Battlefield when the Contested status is applied to a Battlefield with no current controller.

### [official-rule] 316.5.b.1 - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316.5.b
- section_id: 316
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Showdowns that occur as a result of a player moving to an empty Battlefield are a stand-alone Phase and do not create a Combat.

### [official-rule] 316.5.b.1.a - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316.5.b.1
- section_id: 316
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> These Showdowns are called Non-Combat Showdowns. During the proceedings of a Non-Combat Showdown, units controlled by a different player may become present at the Battlefield where the Showdown is ongoing. This will cause the Showdown to become a Combat Showdown.

### [official-rule] 316.5.c - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316.5
- section_id: 316
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Showdown is a structured Window of Opportunity where Players may play cards and activate abilities with Action or Reaction. See rule 341. Showdowns for more information.

### [official-rule] 316.6 - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316
- section_id: 316
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When a player has no more Discretionary Actions they wish to execute, they must indicate they are ending their turn.

### [official-rule] 316.6.a - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316.6
- section_id: 316
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> This ends the Main Phase.

### [official-rule] 316.6.b - Main Phase

- parent_rule: 316.6
- section_id: 316
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Play proceeds to the Ending Phase.

### [official-rule] 317 - Ending Phase

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 317
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Ending Phase

### [official-rule] 317.1 - Ending Phase

- parent_rule: 317
- section_id: 317
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Ending Step

### [official-rule] 317.1.a - Ending Phase

- parent_rule: 317.1
- section_id: 317
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> At the end of the turn Game Effects take place.

### [official-rule] 317.1.b - Ending Phase

- parent_rule: 317.1
- section_id: 317
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Specific game effects and abilities will reference this timing and phase as necessary.

### [official-rule] 317.2 - Ending Phase

- parent_rule: 317
- section_id: 317
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Expiration Step

### [official-rule] 317.2.a - Ending Phase

- parent_rule: 317.2
- section_id: 317
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Invoke an Ending Special Cleanup. See rule 318. Cleanups for more information.

### [official-rule] 317.2.b - Ending Phase

- parent_rule: 317.2
- section_id: 317
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Insert “2c. Heal all Units.”

### [official-rule] 317.2.c - Ending Phase

- parent_rule: 317.2
- section_id: 317
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Insert “2d. All ‘this turn’ effects expire simultaneously.”

### [official-rule] 317.2.d - Ending Phase

- parent_rule: 317.2
- section_id: 317
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Insert “2e. Each player’s Rune Pool empties. Any unspent Energy and Power are lost.”

### [official-rule] 317.2.e - Ending Phase

- parent_rule: 317.2
- section_id: 317
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The following Task becomes Outstanding:

### [official-rule] 317.2.f - Ending Phase

- parent_rule: 317.2
- section_id: 317
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 1. If any items underwent the FEPR process, return to the start of the Expiration Step

### [official-rule] 317.3 - Ending Phase

- parent_rule: 317
- section_id: 317
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The next player with their Turn queued becomes the Turn Player.

### [official-rule] 318 - Cleanups

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 318
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Cleanups

### [official-rule] 319 - A Cleanup will be made an Outstanding Task at the following times:

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 319
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Cleanup will be made an Outstanding Task at the following times:

### [official-rule] 319.1 - A Cleanup will be made an Outstanding Task at the following times:

- parent_rule: 319
- section_id: 319
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> After the game transitions to or from an Open or Closed state

### [official-rule] 319.2 - A Cleanup will be made an Outstanding Task at the following times:

- parent_rule: 319
- section_id: 319
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> After the game transitions between Phases, unless specified otherwise

### [official-rule] 319.3 - A Cleanup will be made an Outstanding Task at the following times:

- parent_rule: 319
- section_id: 319
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> After a Pending Item is added to the Chain

### [official-rule] 319.4 - A Cleanup will be made an Outstanding Task at the following times:

- parent_rule: 319
- section_id: 319
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> After a Pending Item becomes a Legal Item on the Chain

### [official-rule] 319.5 - A Cleanup will be made an Outstanding Task at the following times:

- parent_rule: 319
- section_id: 319
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> After a Chain Item is removed from the Chain for any reason

### [official-rule] 319.6 - A Cleanup will be made an Outstanding Task at the following times:

- parent_rule: 319
- section_id: 319
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> After any number of Game Objects enter or leave the Board

### [official-rule] 319.7 - A Cleanup will be made an Outstanding Task at the following times:

- parent_rule: 319
- section_id: 319
- page: 26
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> After the status of any number of Game Objects changes for any reason

### [official-rule] 319.8 - A Cleanup will be made an Outstanding Task at the following times:

- parent_rule: 319
- section_id: 319
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> After a Move is completed

### [official-rule] 320 - While a Cleanup is occurring, Chain Items cannot be Resolved.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 320
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> While a Cleanup is occurring, Chain Items cannot be Resolved.

### [official-rule] 320.1 - New Pending Items can be added, but Legal Items cannot be executed and Priority and Foc...

- parent_rule: 320
- section_id: 320
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> New Pending Items can be added, but Legal Items cannot be executed and Priority and Focus are not passed or awarded.

### [official-rule] 321 - Similarly, while Chain Items are Resolving, a Cleanup cannot occur.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 321
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Similarly, while Chain Items are Resolving, a Cleanup cannot occur.

### [official-rule] 321.1 - If an event occurs during the Resolution of a Chain Item that qualifies for a Cleanup,...

- parent_rule: 321
- section_id: 321
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If an event occurs during the Resolution of a Chain Item that qualifies for a Cleanup, that Cleanup will be made an Outstanding Task.

### [official-rule] 322 - If an event occurs during a Cleanup that qualifies for a Cleanup, another Cleanup will...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 322
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If an event occurs during a Cleanup that qualifies for a Cleanup, another Cleanup will occur immediately after the first completes, repeating until a Cleanup occurs with no new change in the game’s state.

### [official-rule] 322.1 - These new Cleanups are themselves Outstanding Tasks.

- parent_rule: 322
- section_id: 322
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> These new Cleanups are themselves Outstanding Tasks.

### [official-rule] 323 - When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 323
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

### [official-rule] 323.1 - When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

- parent_rule: 323
- section_id: 323
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 1. If a player has points greater than or equal to the Victory Score, and more points than any opponent, that player wins.

### [official-rule] 323.2 - When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

- parent_rule: 323
- section_id: 323
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 2. Assign or Remove the Attacker or Defender designation from Units as needed if there is a Combat in progress

### [official-rule] 323.2.a - When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

- parent_rule: 323.2
- section_id: 323
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If there are Units present at the Battlefield the Combat is taking place at, but do not have a designation, they gain the same designation as their Controller now

### [official-rule] 323.2.b - When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

- parent_rule: 323.2
- section_id: 323
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If there are Units present at the Battlefield the Combat is taking place at, but have the opposite designation of their controller, they lose that designation, and gain the same designation as their controller now

### [official-rule] 323.2.c - When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

- parent_rule: 323.2
- section_id: 323
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If there are Units at locations other than the Battlefield that the Combat is taking place at, but have either Attacker or Defender designations, they lose those designations now

### [official-rule] 323.3 - When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

- parent_rule: 323
- section_id: 323
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 3. Handle outstanding board state

### [official-rule] 323.4 - When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

- parent_rule: 323
- section_id: 323
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 3a. All Units that have non-zero Damage marked on them equalling or exceeding their Might that have Deathknell abilities will trigger their Deathknell ability now, making note of their current location, attributes, and other information relevant to add the trigger as a Pending Item See rule 808 Deathknell for more information.

### [official-rule] 323.5 - When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

- parent_rule: 323
- section_id: 323
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 3b. All Units that have non-zero Damage marked on them equalling or exceeding their Might are killed and placed in their owners' Trash.

### [official-rule] 323.6 - When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

- parent_rule: 323
- section_id: 323
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 4. If the turn is in an Open State, Battlefields with no Units occupying them and no Showdown or Combat ongoing become Uncontrolled.

### [official-rule] 323.7 - When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

- parent_rule: 323
- section_id: 323
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 5. Recall all Unattached non-Unit Gear at Battlefields, and all Permanents and Runes in Bases other than their controller’s. Remove all Hidden cards from all Battlefields that are not controlled by the same player and place them in their owner's Trash.

### [official-rule] 323.8 - When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

- parent_rule: 323
- section_id: 323
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 6. Mark a Showdown as Staged at each Battlefield that Contested was applied to.

### [official-rule] 323.8.a - When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

- parent_rule: 323.8
- section_id: 323
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The Showdown remains Staged at each Battlefield that is Contested and has units present controlled by the player that applied Contested.

### [official-rule] 323.9 - When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

- parent_rule: 323
- section_id: 323
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 7. Mark a Combat as Staged at each Battlefield that Contested was applied to that have Units present controlled by opposing players.

### [official-rule] 323.9.a - When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

- parent_rule: 323.9
- section_id: 323
- page: 27
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The Combat remains Staged at that Battlefield as long as there are Units present from two opposing players there.

### [official-rule] 323.10 - When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

- parent_rule: 323
- section_id: 323
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 7a. If Units of two opposing players are no longer present at a Battlefield that has a Combat Staged before it has opened, the Combat will cease being Staged

### [official-rule] 323.11 - When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

- parent_rule: 323
- section_id: 323
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 8. If the current state is a Neutral Open State and one or more Showdowns are Staged, the Turn Player chooses one of those Battlefields. A Showdown begins there.

### [official-rule] 323.12 - When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

- parent_rule: 323
- section_id: 323
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 8a. If a Showdown and Combat are staged at the same Battlefield and the turn player chooses to initiate the Showdown there, the Showdown will open as a Combat Showdown.

### [official-rule] 323.13 - When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

- parent_rule: 323
- section_id: 323
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 9. If the current state is a Neutral Open State and Combat is Staged at one or more Battlefields, the Turn Player chooses one of those Battlefields. Combat begins there.

### [official-rule] 323.14 - When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:

- parent_rule: 323
- section_id: 323
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 9a. If the current state is Showdown Open State and Combat is Staged at a Battlefield where there is a Non-Combat Showdown ongoing, that Showdown becomes a Combat Showdown.

### [official-rule] 324 - Special Cleanups are Cleanup steps invoked at specific times that have additional steps...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 324
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Special Cleanups are Cleanup steps invoked at specific times that have additional steps not present in a normal Cleanup.

### [official-rule] 324.1 - When a Special Cleanup is invoked, the unique steps added will be inserted and defined...

- parent_rule: 324
- section_id: 324
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When a Special Cleanup is invoked, the unique steps added will be inserted and defined by the sub-section that invokes it. Example: When a Combat Cleanup is invoked, the Combat section defines what steps are added to the Cleanup. See rule 461. The Resolution Step for more information. Example: When an End of Turn Cleanup is invoked, the End of Turn Phase subsection defines what steps are added to the Cleanup. See rule 317. The Ending Phase for more information.

### [official-rule] 324.2 - If events during a Special Cleanup require another Cleanup, a normal Cleanup is invoked...

- parent_rule: 324
- section_id: 324
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If events during a Special Cleanup require another Cleanup, a normal Cleanup is invoked, not another iteration of the Special Cleanup.

### [official-rule] 325 - Chains and Showdowns

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 325
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Chains and Showdowns

### [official-rule] 326 - Players can act during the following Windows of Opportunity that occur during the cours...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 326
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Players can act during the following Windows of Opportunity that occur during the course of regular play:

### [official-rule] 326.1 - During a Chain

- parent_rule: 326
- section_id: 326
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> During a Chain

### [official-rule] 326.2 - During a Showdown

- parent_rule: 326
- section_id: 326
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> During a Showdown

### [official-rule] 327 - Chains

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 327
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Chains

### [official-rule] 328 - The Chain is a Non-Board Zone that temporarily exists whenever a card is played or an a...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 328
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The Chain is a Non-Board Zone that temporarily exists whenever a card is played or an ability is activated.

### [official-rule] 328.1 - Cards are placed here as part of the process of being played.

- parent_rule: 328
- section_id: 328
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Cards are placed here as part of the process of being played.

### [official-rule] 328.2 - Abilities are queued here as part of the process of resolving. See rule 360. Abilities...

- parent_rule: 328
- section_id: 328
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Abilities are queued here as part of the process of resolving. See rule 360. Abilities for more information.

### [official-rule] 329 - Cards and abilities added to the chain are added as Pending Chain Items that become Fin...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 329
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Cards and abilities added to the chain are added as Pending Chain Items that become Finalized Chain Items.

### [official-rule] 329.1 - Pending Items are on the Chain.

- parent_rule: 329
- section_id: 329
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Pending Items are on the Chain.

### [official-rule] 329.2 - Chain Items are Pending until the “Check Legality” step of playing a card. See rule 349...

- parent_rule: 329
- section_id: 329
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Chain Items are Pending until the “Check Legality” step of playing a card. See rule 349. Playing Cards for more information.

### [official-rule] 329.3 - When a Pending Chain Item is no longer Pending it is finalized and becomes a Finalized...

- parent_rule: 329
- section_id: 329
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When a Pending Chain Item is no longer Pending it is finalized and becomes a Finalized Chain Item.

### [official-rule] 330 - The Chain exists as long as a Chain Item is on it.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 330
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The Chain exists as long as a Chain Item is on it.

### [official-rule] 330.1 - Only one Chain can exist at a time.

- parent_rule: 330
- section_id: 330
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Only one Chain can exist at a time.

### [official-rule] 330.2 - If a card would begin to be played while a Chain already exists, it is placed on the ex...

- parent_rule: 330
- section_id: 330
- page: 28
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If a card would begin to be played while a Chain already exists, it is placed on the existing Chain.

### [official-rule] 331 - The State of the Turn is partially determined by whether or not the Chain currently exi...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 331
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The State of the Turn is partially determined by whether or not the Chain currently exists.

### [official-rule] 331.1 - The turn is said to be in a Closed State if a Chain exists.

- parent_rule: 331
- section_id: 331
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The turn is said to be in a Closed State if a Chain exists.

### [official-rule] 331.1.a - Cards of all Categories, by default, cannot be played during a Closed State.

- parent_rule: 331.1
- section_id: 331
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Cards of all Categories, by default, cannot be played during a Closed State.

### [official-rule] 331.1.b - Card abilities, by default, cannot be played during a Closed State.

- parent_rule: 331.1
- section_id: 331
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Card abilities, by default, cannot be played during a Closed State.

### [official-rule] 331.2 - The turn is said to be in an Open State if no Chain exists.

- parent_rule: 331
- section_id: 331
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The turn is said to be in an Open State if no Chain exists.

### [official-rule] 332 - Steps of Resolving Chain Items

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 332
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Steps of Resolving Chain Items

### [official-rule] 333 - Whenever a card or token is played or an ability is activated or triggered, a Chain is...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 333
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Whenever a card or token is played or an ability is activated or triggered, a Chain is created.

### [official-rule] 333.1 - The player that created the chain becomes the first player with Priority.

- parent_rule: 333
- section_id: 333
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The player that created the chain becomes the first player with Priority.

### [official-rule] 334 - A Task is one or more steps or processes that one or more Players must perform before c...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 334
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Task is one or more steps or processes that one or more Players must perform before continuing with any other actions.

### [official-rule] 334.1 - Tasks include, but are not limited to: Cleanups, the actions performed during the Start...

- parent_rule: 334
- section_id: 334
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Tasks include, but are not limited to: Cleanups, the actions performed during the Start of Turn Process, throughout Combat in its various steps, and the actions performed during the End of Turn Process. See rule 318. Cleanups for more information on Cleanups See rule 315. Start of Turn for more information on the Start of Turn process See rule 454. Combat for more information on the steps of Combat See rule 317. Ending Phase for more information.

### [official-rule] 335 - Whenever a Player takes one or more actions that incur Tasks they should refer to the p...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 335
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Whenever a Player takes one or more actions that incur Tasks they should refer to the process of HOT FEPR: Handle Outstanding Tasks; then Finalize, Execute, Pass, Resolve.

### [official-rule] 335.1 - In the course of Handling Outstanding Tasks, Chain Items may be added to the Chain. The...

- parent_rule: 335
- section_id: 335
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> In the course of Handling Outstanding Tasks, Chain Items may be added to the Chain. They will remain there until the Tasks are complete.

### [official-rule] 335.2 - When all Outstanding Tasks are completed, all pending Chain Items will subsequently be...

- parent_rule: 335
- section_id: 335
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When all Outstanding Tasks are completed, all pending Chain Items will subsequently be processed by the FEPR process.

### [official-rule] 335.2.a - During the FEPR process, new Tasks may be incurred. Pause the process and complete the...

- parent_rule: 335.2
- section_id: 335
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> During the FEPR process, new Tasks may be incurred. Pause the process and complete the necessary Tasks before continuing.

### [official-rule] 335.3 - If there are no Outstanding Tasks, no pending Chain Items, and it is the Main Phase, th...

- parent_rule: 335
- section_id: 335
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If there are no Outstanding Tasks, no pending Chain Items, and it is the Main Phase, the Turn Player receives priority. If there are no Outstanding Tasks, no pending items, and it is any other phase of the turn, proceed to the next substep, step, phase, or turn.

### [official-rule] 336 - When there are no outstanding Tasks and there are pending Chain Items on the Chain, pla...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 336
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When there are no outstanding Tasks and there are pending Chain Items on the Chain, players should refer to the FEPR process to proceed.

### [official-rule] 336.1 - In the sequence of resolving FEPR more Chain Items may become Pending Chain Items. Thes...

- parent_rule: 336
- section_id: 336
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> In the sequence of resolving FEPR more Chain Items may become Pending Chain Items. These will be processed by the same FEPR process that produced them.

### [official-rule] 337 - Step 1: Finalize

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 337
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Step 1: Finalize

### [official-rule] 337.1 - Step 1: Finalize

- parent_rule: 337
- section_id: 337
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If one or more Items are Pending, their controllers must complete the steps of Playing those Pending Items until they are Finalized Items or leave the Chain.

### [official-rule] 337.1.a - Step 1: Finalize

- parent_rule: 337.1
- section_id: 337
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> This process does not pass Priority.

### [official-rule] 337.1.b - Step 1: Finalize

- parent_rule: 337.1
- section_id: 337
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Each Item is Finalized in the order it was appended to the Chain.

### [official-rule] 337.1.c - Step 1: Finalize

- parent_rule: 337.1
- section_id: 337
- page: 29
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Units, Gear, and abilities that Add resources resolve immediately when Finalized and do not progress to Step 2: Execute. See rule 349. Playing Cards for more information.

### [official-rule] 337.1.c.1 - Step 1: Finalize

- parent_rule: 337.1.c
- section_id: 337
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the Chain is empty, play proceeds in an Open State.

### [official-rule] 337.1.c.1.a - Step 1: Finalize

- parent_rule: 337.1.c.1
- section_id: 337
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If this occurs during a Showdown, the next player in turn order gains Focus.

### [official-rule] 337.1.c.2 - Step 1: Finalize

- parent_rule: 337.1.c
- section_id: 337
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the Chain is not empty and there are one or more Pending Items, repeat Step 1.

### [official-rule] 337.1.c.3 - Step 1: Finalize

- parent_rule: 337.1.c
- section_id: 337
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the Chain is not empty and there are no Pending Items, the controller of the newest item on the chain gains Priority and becomes the Active Player. Proceed to Step 2: Execute.

### [official-rule] 338 - Step 2: Execute

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 338
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Step 2: Execute

### [official-rule] 338.1 - Step 2: Execute

- parent_rule: 338
- section_id: 338
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The player with Priority may do any the following:

### [official-rule] 338.1.a - Step 2: Execute

- parent_rule: 338.1
- section_id: 338
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Play a Card that is legally timed.

### [official-rule] 338.1.a.1 - Step 2: Execute

- parent_rule: 338.1.a
- section_id: 338
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Cards, by default, cannot be played during a Closed State.

### [official-rule] 338.1.a.2 - Step 2: Execute

- parent_rule: 338.1.a
- section_id: 338
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Legally Timed Card would be a Card with Reaction or a card that will have Reaction when played under appropriate circumstances.

### [official-rule] 338.1.a.3 - Step 2: Execute

- parent_rule: 338.1.a
- section_id: 338
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Other exceptions may be created during regular play.

### [official-rule] 338.1.a.4 - Step 2: Execute

- parent_rule: 338.1.a
- section_id: 338
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The card will be added to the chain as a Pending Item, following the steps of playing a card.

### [official-rule] 338.1.a.5 - Step 2: Execute

- parent_rule: 338.1.a
- section_id: 338
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> This can be an additional card to the item that Started the Chain in the case of the first player with Priority after creating the Chain.

### [official-rule] 338.1.a.6 - Step 2: Execute

- parent_rule: 338.1.a
- section_id: 338
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Whether a Card is legally timed is evaluated during the “Check Legality” step of Playing a Card. See rule 349. Playing Cards for more information.

### [official-rule] 338.1.a.7 - Step 2: Execute

- parent_rule: 338.1.a
- section_id: 338
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Playing a card will create one or more Pending Items. Return to Step 1: Finalize.

### [official-rule] 338.1.b - Step 2: Execute

- parent_rule: 338.1
- section_id: 338
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Activate Abilities of Game Objects that are legally timed. See rule 398. Playing or Activating Abilities for more information.

### [official-rule] 338.1.b.1 - Step 2: Execute

- parent_rule: 338.1.b
- section_id: 338
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> All rules for legally timed cards apply to legally timed abilities.

### [official-rule] 338.1.b.2 - Step 2: Execute

- parent_rule: 338.1.b
- section_id: 338
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Activating abilities will create one or more Pending Items. Return to Step 1: Finalize.

### [official-rule] 338.1.c - Step 2: Execute

- parent_rule: 338.1
- section_id: 338
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Pass Priority

### [official-rule] 338.1.c.1 - Step 2: Execute

- parent_rule: 338.1.c
- section_id: 338
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The player with Priority passes Priority to the next Player in Turn Order. Proceed to Step 3: Pass.

### [official-rule] 339 - Step 3: Pass

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 339
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Step 3: Pass

### [official-rule] 339.1 - Step 3: Pass

- parent_rule: 339
- section_id: 339
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If all players have passed Priority without adding any items to the Chain, proceed to Step 4: Resolve.

### [official-rule] 339.2 - Step 3: Pass

- parent_rule: 339
- section_id: 339
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Otherwise, the player with Priority passes Priority to the next Player in Turn Order. Return to Step 3: Execute.

### [official-rule] 340 - Step 4: Resolve

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 340
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Step 4: Resolve

### [official-rule] 340.1 - Step 4: Resolve

- parent_rule: 340
- section_id: 340
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The newest item on the Chain resolves. Execute its game effects in their entirety. See rule 349. Playing Cards for more information on resolving spells. See rule 398. Playing or Activating Abilities for more information on resolving abilities.

### [official-rule] 340.2 - Step 4: Resolve

- parent_rule: 340
- section_id: 340
- page: 30
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the Chain is empty, play proceeds in an Open State.

### [official-rule] 340.2.a - Step 4: Resolve

- parent_rule: 340.2
- section_id: 340
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If this occurs during a Showdown, focus passes to the next player in turn order.

### [official-rule] 340.3 - Step 4: Resolve

- parent_rule: 340
- section_id: 340
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the Chain is not empty and there are one or more Pending Items, return to Step 1: Finalize.

### [official-rule] 340.4 - Step 4: Resolve

- parent_rule: 340
- section_id: 340
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the Chain is not empty and there are no Pending Items, the controller of the newest item on the chain gains Priority. Return to Step 2: Execute.

### [official-rule] 341 - Showdowns

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 341
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Showdowns

### [official-rule] 342 - A Showdown is a Window of Opportunity in which Players have an Open State in which they...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 342
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Showdown is a Window of Opportunity in which Players have an Open State in which they may play Spells in an alternating fashion.

### [official-rule] 342.1 - Each spell played this way creates a Chain as normal.

- parent_rule: 342
- section_id: 342
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Each spell played this way creates a Chain as normal.

### [official-rule] 343 - The State of the turn is partially determined by whether or not a Showdown or Combat is...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 343
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The State of the turn is partially determined by whether or not a Showdown or Combat is in progress.

### [official-rule] 343.1 - The turn is said to be in a Showdown State if a Showdown or Combat is in progress.

- parent_rule: 343
- section_id: 343
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The turn is said to be in a Showdown State if a Showdown or Combat is in progress.

### [official-rule] 343.1.a - Cards of all Categories, by default, cannot be played during a Showdown State.

- parent_rule: 343.1
- section_id: 343
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Cards of all Categories, by default, cannot be played during a Showdown State.

### [official-rule] 343.1.b - Card abilities, by default, cannot be played during a Showdown State.

- parent_rule: 343.1
- section_id: 343
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Card abilities, by default, cannot be played during a Showdown State.

### [official-rule] 343.2 - The turn is said to be in a Neutral State if no Showdown or Combat is in progress.

- parent_rule: 343
- section_id: 343
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The turn is said to be in a Neutral State if no Showdown or Combat is in progress.

### [official-rule] 344 - A Showdown begins when Control of a Battlefield is Contested and the turn is in a Neutr...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 344
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Showdown begins when Control of a Battlefield is Contested and the turn is in a Neutral Open State.

### [official-rule] 344.1 - If Control of a Battlefield is Contested between two players, then a Showdown will be o...

- parent_rule: 344
- section_id: 344
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If Control of a Battlefield is Contested between two players, then a Showdown will be opened as the first step of Combat. See rule 454. Combat for more information.

### [official-rule] 344.2 - If Control of a Battlefield is Contested and the Battlefield in question is uncontrolle...

- parent_rule: 344
- section_id: 344
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If Control of a Battlefield is Contested and the Battlefield in question is uncontrolled when it becomes Contested, a Showdown is opened during the Cleanup at the end of the action that caused the Battlefield to become Contested.

### [official-rule] 345 - As a Showdown begins, the player who applied Contested status to the Battlefield gains...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 345
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> As a Showdown begins, the player who applied Contested status to the Battlefield gains Focus.

### [official-rule] 346 - When the last item on the chain resolves and the turn returns to an Open State during a...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 346
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When the last item on the chain resolves and the turn returns to an Open State during a Showdown, Focus passes, and the next Player gains both Focus and Priority.

### [official-rule] 346.1 - Focus will not pass in this way if the chain opened as a result of a triggered ability...

- parent_rule: 346
- section_id: 346
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Focus will not pass in this way if the chain opened as a result of a triggered ability being added to the chain, nor if it opened as a result of an Add ability being added to the chain. Example: the Initial Chain opens as a result of triggered abilities being added to the chain, so when the last item on the Initial Chain resolves and the turn returns to an Open State, Focus will not pass.

### [official-rule] 347 - During a Showdown, the player with Focus may do one of the following:

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 347
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> During a Showdown, the player with Focus may do one of the following:

### [official-rule] 347.1 - During a Showdown, the player with Focus may do one of the following:

- parent_rule: 347
- section_id: 347
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Play a card that is legally timed.

### [official-rule] 347.1.a - During a Showdown, the player with Focus may do one of the following:

- parent_rule: 347.1
- section_id: 347
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The card will start a Chain as normal.

### [official-rule] 347.2 - During a Showdown, the player with Focus may do one of the following:

- parent_rule: 347
- section_id: 347
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Activate Abilities of Game Objects that are legally timed. See rule 360. Abilities for more information.

### [official-rule] 347.3 - During a Showdown, the player with Focus may do one of the following:

- parent_rule: 347
- section_id: 347
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Pass.

### [official-rule] 347.3.a - During a Showdown, the player with Focus may do one of the following:

- parent_rule: 347.3
- section_id: 347
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If all Players have passed once in sequence, the Showdown ends.

### [official-rule] 347.3.a.1 - During a Showdown, the player with Focus may do one of the following:

- parent_rule: 347.3.a
- section_id: 347
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Perform a Cleanup.

### [official-rule] 347.4 - During a Showdown, the player with Focus may do one of the following:

- parent_rule: 347
- section_id: 347
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Otherwise, Focus passes to the next Player in Turn Order.

### [official-rule] 348 - If all players pass Focus without playing a spell or activating an ability, then the Sh...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 348
- page: 31
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If all players pass Focus without playing a spell or activating an ability, then the Showdown Closes.

### [official-rule] 348.1 - If it is a Combat Showdown, proceed with the remaining steps of Combat to resolve the p...

- parent_rule: 348
- section_id: 348
- page: 32
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If it is a Combat Showdown, proceed with the remaining steps of Combat to resolve the phase. See rule 458. The Steps of Combat for more information.

### [official-rule] 348.2 - If it is a Non-Combat Showdown, do the following:

- parent_rule: 348
- section_id: 348
- page: 32
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If it is a Non-Combat Showdown, do the following:

### [official-rule] 348.2.a - If only one player’s Units remain at the Battlefield, and if that player does not alrea...

- parent_rule: 348.2
- section_id: 348
- page: 32
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If only one player’s Units remain at the Battlefield, and if that player does not already Control the Battlefield, that player establishes Control over the Battlefield. See rule 185. Control for more information on Control. See rule 464.1. for more information on Conquering.

### [official-rule] 348.2.a.1 - This results in a Conquer if that player has not yet scored that Battlefield this turn.

- parent_rule: 348.2.a
- section_id: 348
- page: 32
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> This results in a Conquer if that player has not yet scored that Battlefield this turn.

### [official-rule] 349 - Playing Cards

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 349
- page: 32
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Playing Cards

### [official-rule] 350 - Playing a card is the act of a player utilizing their cards.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 350
- page: 32
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Playing a card is the act of a player utilizing their cards.

### [official-rule] 350.1 - A card is Played when it has finished this process in its entirety.

- parent_rule: 350
- section_id: 350
- page: 32
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A card is Played when it has finished this process in its entirety.

### [official-rule] 350.2 - Tokens are not cards, but can still be Played. See rule 176. Tokens for more information.

- parent_rule: 350
- section_id: 350
- page: 32
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Tokens are not cards, but can still be Played. See rule 176. Tokens for more information.

### [official-rule] 351 - Cards have different behaviors when played.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 351
- page: 32
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Cards have different behaviors when played.

### [official-rule] 351.1 - Permanents become Game Objects when Played.

- parent_rule: 351
- section_id: 351
- page: 32
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Permanents become Game Objects when Played.

### [official-rule] 351.2 - Spells create game effects that are executed, then the card is placed in the trash when...

- parent_rule: 351
- section_id: 351
- page: 32
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Spells create game effects that are executed, then the card is placed in the trash when Played.

### [official-rule] 352 - Cards have different states during the process of being played.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 352
- page: 32
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Cards have different states during the process of being played.

### [official-rule] 352.1 - When initially being played cards are Pending, as Pending Chain Items.

- parent_rule: 352
- section_id: 352
- page: 32
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When initially being played cards are Pending, as Pending Chain Items.

### [official-rule] 352.2 - Near the end of the process cards will cease being Pending and become Finalized Chain I...

- parent_rule: 352
- section_id: 352
- page: 32
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:
