> Step 3: The Resolution Step

### [official-rule] 461.1 - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461
- section_id: 461
- page: 71
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 1. Perform a Combat Cleanup.

### [official-rule] 461.1.a - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461.1
- section_id: 461
- page: 71
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Invoke a Combat Special Cleanup.

### [official-rule] 461.1.a.1 - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461.1.a
- section_id: 461
- page: 71
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Insert “3c. Heal all Units.”

### [official-rule] 461.1.a.2 - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461.1.a
- section_id: 461
- page: 71
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Insert “3d. Recall Attackers present at the Battlefield if Defenders are still present.” See rule 449. Recalls for more information.

### [official-rule] 461.2 - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461
- section_id: 461
- page: 71
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The following Task becomes Outstanding:

### [official-rule] 461.3 - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461
- section_id: 461
- page: 71
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 1. Determine Combat Result

### [official-rule] 461.3.a - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461.3
- section_id: 461
- page: 71
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Player has won a combat if they received either the attacker or defender designation and are the only Player that has units remaining at this battlefield during this step.

### [official-rule] 461.3.b - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461.3
- section_id: 461
- page: 71-72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Player has lost a combat if they received either the attacker or defender designation and are the only Player that does not have any units remaining at this battlefield during this step.

### [official-rule] 461.3.c - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461.3
- section_id: 461
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Units at this battlefield inherit the same combat result as their controllers

### [official-rule] 461.3.d - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461.3
- section_id: 461
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> There is “No Result” if either both Players have units present during this step, or neither player has units present during this step.

### [official-rule] 461.3.d.1 - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461.3.d
- section_id: 461
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If “No Result” was reached, and both players have units remaining, stage a Combat at this battlefield.

### [official-rule] 461 - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 461
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

### [official-rule] 461.5 - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461
- section_id: 461
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 1. If no Showdown or Combat is staged at this location, the player with Units remaining here Establishes Control.

### [official-rule] 461.5.a - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461.5
- section_id: 461
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Clear the Contested Status.

### [official-rule] 461.5.b - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461.5
- section_id: 461
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If there are no Units remaining here controlled by any player, the Battlefield becomes Uncontrolled.

### [official-rule] 461.5.c - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461.5
- section_id: 461
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Remove all Hidden cards from this Battlefield that do not share a controller with the Battlefield.

### [official-rule] 461.5.d - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461.5
- section_id: 461
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Establishing Control results in a Conquer if that player has not yet scored this Battlefield this turn. See rule 185. Control for more information on Control. See rule 464.1. for more information on Conquering.

### [official-rule] 461.5.e - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461.5
- section_id: 461
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> This does not have to be the player that applied Contested to the Battlefield.

### [official-rule] 461.6 - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461
- section_id: 461
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The following Task becomes Outstanding:

### [official-rule] 461.7 - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461
- section_id: 461
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 1. Combat ends.

### [official-rule] 461.7.a - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461.7
- section_id: 461
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Remove Attacker and Defender Designation from all Units and Players.

### [official-rule] 461.7 - 4 The following Task becomes Outstanding:

- parent_rule: 461
- section_id: 461
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> b All “this combat” effects expire simultaneously.

### [official-rule] 462 - Scoring

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 462
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Scoring

### [official-rule] 463 - Scoring is the act of a Player gaining a point through the process of seizing or mainta...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 463
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Scoring is the act of a Player gaining a point through the process of seizing or maintaining control over Battlefields.

### [official-rule] 463.1 - Every instance of Scoring is also an instance of Gaining points

- parent_rule: 463
- section_id: 463
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Every instance of Scoring is also an instance of Gaining points

### [official-rule] 464 - A player Scores in one of two ways:

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 464
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A player Scores in one of two ways:

### [official-rule] 464.1 - A player Scores in one of two ways:

- parent_rule: 464
- section_id: 464
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Conquer: A player gains Control of a Battlefield they did not yet Score this turn.

### [official-rule] 464.1.a - A player Scores in one of two ways:

- parent_rule: 464.1
- section_id: 464
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> In Modes of Play with teammates, Battlefields under the Control of a teammate during the Beginning Phase are also disqualified from being Scored through Conquer by any means.

### [official-rule] 464.1.b - A player Scores in one of two ways:

- parent_rule: 464.1
- section_id: 464
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A player will gain control of a Battlefield after establishing Control by applying Contested first.

### [official-rule] 464.2 - A player Scores in one of two ways:

- parent_rule: 464
- section_id: 464
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Hold: A player maintains Control of a Battlefield during their Beginning Phase.

### [official-rule] 465 - A player may only Score, from either method, once per Battlefield per turn.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 465
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A player may only Score, from either method, once per Battlefield per turn.

### [official-rule] 466 - When a player Scores, two things occur:

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 466
- page: 72
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When a player Scores, two things occur:

### [official-rule] 466.1 - When a player Scores, two things occur:

- parent_rule: 466
- section_id: 466
- page: 73
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The player Gains up to one Point, depending on their current score.

### [official-rule] 466.1.a - When a player Scores, two things occur:

- parent_rule: 466.1
- section_id: 466
- page: 73
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The Winning Point has additional restrictions.

### [official-rule] 466.1.a.1 - When a player Scores, two things occur:

- parent_rule: 466.1.a
- section_id: 466
- page: 73
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Notably, points Gained from sources that are not Conquer or Hold are not beholden to these restrictions.

### [official-rule] 466.1.b - When a player Scores, two things occur:

- parent_rule: 466.1
- section_id: 466
- page: 73
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When a player tries to Gain a Point through a Score, and their current Point Total is 1 point from the Victory Score of the Mode of Play or higher, the following occurs:

### [official-rule] 466.1.b.1 - When a player Scores, two things occur:

- parent_rule: 466.1.b
- section_id: 466
- page: 73
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the player has Scored through Hold, that player Gains the Winning Point.

### [official-rule] 466.1.b.2 - When a player Scores, two things occur:

- parent_rule: 466.1.b
- section_id: 466
- page: 73
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the player has Scored through a Conquer and has Scored every Battlefield through either method this turn, that player Gains the Winning Point. If the player has Scored through a Conquer and has not Scored every Battlefield this turn, that player draws a card.

### [official-rule] 466.2 - When a player Scores, two things occur:

- parent_rule: 466
- section_id: 466
- page: 73
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Trigger Score abilities at the Battlefield that Scored.

### [official-rule] 466.2.a - When a player Scores, two things occur:

- parent_rule: 466.2
- section_id: 466
- page: 73
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Conquer abilities trigger at a Battlefield that was Conquered.

### [official-rule] 466.2.b - When a player Scores, two things occur:

- parent_rule: 466.2
- section_id: 466
- page: 73
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Hold abilities trigger at a Battlefield that was Held.

### [official-rule] 466.2.c - When a player Scores, two things occur:

- parent_rule: 466.2
- section_id: 466
- page: 73
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> These will only trigger when the Battlefield is Scored; I.E. These cannot be triggered more than once per turn for a player.

### [official-rule] 467 - When a cleanup occurs and a player has accrued Points greater than or equal to the Vict...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 467
- page: 73
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When a cleanup occurs and a player has accrued Points greater than or equal to the Victory Score for their Mode of Play, and if they have more points than any opponent, they Win the Game.

### [official-rule] 468 - Layers

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 468
- page: 73
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Layers

### [official-rule] 469 - Layers are the mechanism in which Game Effects alter the Traits, Intrinsic Abilities, o...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 469
- page: 73
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Layers are the mechanism in which Game Effects alter the Traits, Intrinsic Abilities, or other properties of Game Objects.

### [official-rule] 470 - Layers are an organizational structure.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 470
- page: 73
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Layers are an organizational structure.

### [official-rule] 470.1 - Layers only serve to structure the application and order that Game Effects apply to Gam...

- parent_rule: 470
- section_id: 470
- page: 73
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Layers only serve to structure the application and order that Game Effects apply to Game Objects to maintain consistency.

### [official-rule] 471 - The layers are applied repeatedly until all effects operating on objects have been appl...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 471
- page: 73
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The layers are applied repeatedly until all effects operating on objects have been applied once and no changes have been processed.

### [official-rule] 471.1 - Layers are applied in sequence. Each effect in them is applied as soon as able, and onl...

- parent_rule: 471
- section_id: 471
- page: 73
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Layers are applied in sequence. Each effect in them is applied as soon as able, and only a single time across all sequences.

### [official-rule] 471.2 - When a sequence of applications completes, recur the process, and evaluate each layer a...

- parent_rule: 471
- section_id: 471
- page: 73
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When a sequence of applications completes, recur the process, and evaluate each layer again applying any effects that may now be applicable.

### [official-rule] 471.3 - The removal or disqualification of an effect is separate from the application of the ef...

- parent_rule: 471
- section_id: 471
- page: 73-74
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The removal or disqualification of an effect is separate from the application of the effect, but still can only be applied once. Example: Fiora, Victorious has printed Might 4 and says “While I'm Mighty, I have Deflect, Ganking, and Shield.” If a player places a buff on Fiora, her Might is increased in the Arithmetic layer, after the layer for Ability-Altering Effects. The Ability-Altering Effect layer is then re-checked and the abilities Deflect, Ganking, and Shield applied. Since each effect has been applied once and there are no other effects to apply, Fiora’s characteristics are finalized as 5 Might with Deflect, Ganking, and Shield. While a buffed Fiora, Victorious is in combat as a defender, an additional +1 Might will be applied in the Arithmetic layer, giving her 6 Might and the 3 keywords. Example: A buffed Fiora, Victorious is in combat as a defender when her buff is removed. Reevaluating the layers in sequence, she no longer gains Deflect, Ganking, and Shield during the Ability-Altering Effect layer, so when the Arithmetic layer is evaluated, neither the buff (which is gone) nor Shield (which she no longer has) apply. She goes directly from 6 Might with three keywords to 4 Might with no keywords.

### [official-rule] 472 - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 472
- page: 74
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Layers are applied in the following order:

### [official-rule] 472.1 - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472
- section_id: 472
- page: 74
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 1. Trait-Altering Effects

### [official-rule] 472.1.a - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472.1
- section_id: 472
- page: 74
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> This layer deals with effects that grant, remove, or replace inherent traits of Game Objects. Name Super Type Type Tags Controller Cost Domain

### [official-rule] 472.1.a.1 - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472.1.a
- section_id: 472
- page: 74
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Assignment of Might is dealt with in this layer. Example: A spell reads "A unit's Might becomes 4 this turn." The unit's Might is set to 4 in this layer.

### [official-rule] 472.1.b - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472.1
- section_id: 472
- page: 74
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Copy effects are applied in this layer.

### [official-rule] 472.1.b.1 - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472.1.b
- section_id: 472
- page: 74
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When one Game Object becomes a copy of another, all Traits, including the Rules Text, replace or are added to those of the original Game Object as specified by the Game Effect directing the Copy. This is applied in this layer.

### [official-rule] 472.1.b.2 - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472.1.b
- section_id: 472
- page: 74
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Some Game Effects may specify copying certain traits of a card - only the traits specified by the Game Effect will be copied.

### [official-rule] 472.1.b.3 - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472.1.b
- section_id: 472
- page: 74
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Copy effects will copy the copyable traits of a Game Object: by default, those are the printed traits of the Game Object. When a Game Object becomes a copy of something, its copyable traits are updated to the new traits it has received. Example: A player triggers Leblanc, Deceiver’s hold effect and plays a Reflection token, making it a copy of Honest Broker. That player then plays Mirror Image, targeting the Reflection token. When the Mirror Image Reflection token is played, it copies all of the copyable traits of the original Reflection token - which are currently those of Honest Broker which it is a copy of. That player will have three units named Honest Broker in play, two of which are token Copies with Temporary.

### [official-rule] 472.1.c - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472.1
- section_id: 472
- page: 74
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Effects for this layer can be identified by the phrase "become(s)", "give," "is," or "are" in the text. Example: A permanent has the ability "Other friendly units are Yordles." Other friendly units gain the Yordle tag in this layer.

### [official-rule] 472.2 - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472
- section_id: 472
- page: 74
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 2. Ability-Altering Effects

### [official-rule] 472.2.a - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472.2
- section_id: 472
- page: 74
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> This layer deals with effects that grant, remove, or replace the abilities or rules text of Game Objects. Keywords Passive Abilities Appending rules text Removing rules text Duplicating Rules Text from one Game Object to another

### [official-rule] 472.2.b - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472.2
- section_id: 472
- page: 74
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Effects for this layer can be identified by the phrase "become(s)," "give," "lose(s)," "have," "has," "is," or "are" in the text. Example: A permanent has the ability "Other friendly units have [Vision]." Other friendly units gain the Vision keyword in this layer.

### [official-rule] 472.2.c - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472.2
- section_id: 472
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Abilities of Effect Text of Attached cards are appended in this layer.

### [official-rule] 472.3 - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472
- section_id: 472
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 3. Arithmetic

### [official-rule] 472.3.a - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472.3
- section_id: 472
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> This layer deals with the mathematics of increasing and decreasing the numeric values of the traits of Game Objects. Might Energy Cost Power Cost

### [official-rule] 472.3.b - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472.3
- section_id: 472
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When an arithmetic effect has a limitation that applies, it is limited at the time of its application, and is “remembered” at that limited level for the duration of its effect. This process is called “snapshotting.” Example: If an effect gives a unit “-4 [M] to a min of 1 this turn” choosing a unit with 2 [M], then the effect will generate -1 [M] this turn.

### [official-rule] 472.3.c - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472.3
- section_id: 472
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Might Bonuses of Attached cards are applied in this layer.

### [official-rule] 472.3.d - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472.3
- section_id: 472
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> This layer applies arithmetic in the following way.

### [official-rule] 472.3.d.1 - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472.3.d
- section_id: 472
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 1. Increases

### [official-rule] 472.3.d.1.a - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472.3.d.1
- section_id: 472
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Positive values, or increases, to Might are applied first.

### [official-rule] 472.3.d.1.b - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472.3.d.1
- section_id: 472
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If there is a restriction or limitation to this increase, the limitation is “snapshotted” for the duration of the effect.

### [official-rule] 472.3.d.2 - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472.3.d
- section_id: 472
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 2. Decreases

### [official-rule] 472.3.d.2.a - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472.3.d.2
- section_id: 472
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Negative values, or decreases, to Might are applied last.

### [official-rule] 472.3.d.2.b - Layers are applied in the following order:

- parent_rule: 472.3.d.2
- section_id: 472
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If there is a restriction or limitation to this decrease, the limitation is snapshotted for the duration of the effect.

### [official-rule] 473 - If more than one effect applies to the same Game Object in the Same Layer, or to each o...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 473
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If more than one effect applies to the same Game Object in the Same Layer, or to each other in the same layer, then both effects will apply but their order may be determined by Dependency.

### [official-rule] 473.1 - A Dependency is established if:

- parent_rule: 473
- section_id: 473
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Dependency is established if:

### [official-rule] 473.1.a - Applying one of the effects alters the existence of the other; or

- parent_rule: 473.1
- section_id: 473
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Applying one of the effects alters the existence of the other; or

### [official-rule] 473.1.b - Applying one of the effects alters the number of objects the other effect can influence

- parent_rule: 473.1
- section_id: 473
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Applying one of the effects alters the number of objects the other effect can influence

### [official-rule] 473.1.c - Applying one of the effects alters the outcome when applying the other.

- parent_rule: 473.1
- section_id: 473
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Applying one of the effects alters the outcome when applying the other.

### [official-rule] 474 - To determine which effect Depends on another, determine which of the prior criteria app...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 474
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> To determine which effect Depends on another, determine which of the prior criteria applies, and then also which effect’s evaluation is altered by the sequence of applications. That effect is said to Depend on the other.

### [official-rule] 474.1 - To resolve a dependency, the effects within the same layer that created the dependency...

- parent_rule: 474
- section_id: 474
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> To resolve a dependency, the effects within the same layer that created the dependency must be applied such that: 1. Identify which effect Depends on the other within the Layer. 2. Apply the effect that is depended on first. 3. Immediately apply the effect that Depends on the first effect next.

### [official-rule] 475 - If more than one effect applies in the same layer but no dependency is established, the...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 475
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If more than one effect applies in the same layer but no dependency is established, then Timestamp order is applied to the effects within that layer and sublayer

### [official-rule] 475.1 - When an effect begins applying, it establishes a time for which it is compared against...

- parent_rule: 475
- section_id: 475
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When an effect begins applying, it establishes a time for which it is compared against other Game Effects for purposes of resolving Layered effects as its Timestamp.

### [official-rule] 475.1.a - Timestamps are not rote values.

- parent_rule: 475.1
- section_id: 475
- page: 75
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Timestamps are not rote values.

### [official-rule] 475.1.b - Timestamps are relative comparisons between effects and when they began applying to the...

- parent_rule: 475.1
- section_id: 475
- page: 76
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Timestamps are relative comparisons between effects and when they began applying to the game.

### [official-rule] 475.1.c - Timestamps are not referenced by Game Effects in any way. They are only used to finaliz...

- parent_rule: 475.1
- section_id: 475
- page: 76
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Timestamps are not referenced by Game Effects in any way. They are only used to finalize layered effects.

### [official-rule] 475.2 - When Rules Text becomes Inactive for any reason, it loses its Timestamp. When it ceases...

- parent_rule: 475
- section_id: 475
- page: 76
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When Rules Text becomes Inactive for any reason, it loses its Timestamp. When it ceases to be Inactive, a new Timestamp is established.

### [official-rule] 475.3 - Effects are applied such that the earliest Timestamp within each Layer and Sublayer app...

- parent_rule: 475
- section_id: 475
- page: 76
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Effects are applied such that the earliest Timestamp within each Layer and Sublayer applies first, followed by other Effects in that Layer and Sublayer in chronological order.

- section_id: 481
- page: 77
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 1v1 (Match)

### [official-rule] 481.1 - 1v1 (Match)

- parent_rule: 481
- section_id: 481
- page: 77
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 2 Players

### [official-rule] 481.2 - 1v1 (Match)

- section_id: 481

- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> 1v1 1 opponent each No teams

### [official-rule] 481.3 - 1v1 (Match)

- parent_rule: 481
- section_id: 481
- page: 77
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Victory Score: 8

### [official-rule] 481.4 - 1v1 (Match)

- parent_rule: 481
- section_id: 481
- page: 77
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Battlefield Count: 2

### [official-rule] 481.4.a - 1v1 (Match)

- parent_rule: 481.4
- section_id: 481
- page: 77
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Each player provides three (3) Battlefields, included in their deck during deck building. Only 1 will be used, chosen during setup.

### [official-rule] 481.5 - 1v1 (Match)

- parent_rule: 481
- section_id: 481
- page: 77
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Setup: Each player selects one (1) of their three (3) Battlefields. The other two are set aside and will not be used for this round of play. The selected Battlefields are placed simultaneously in the Battlefield Zone. After this game, the Battlefields that were used are to be removed and not selected again for this Match. One of the remaining Battlefields that were set aside must be chosen instead.

### [official-rule] 481.6 - 1v1 (Match)

- parent_rule: 481
- section_id: 481
- page: 77
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Format: Best of 3. The first player to reach the Victory Score in Points wins the game. The winner of that game earns One Game Win. Players then reset the game state, remove the Battlefields in play from the game, choose new Battlefields from those set aside, and play again. The first player to earn Two Game Wins wins the match.

### [official-rule] 481.7 - 1v1 (Match)

- parent_rule: 481
- section_id: 481
- page: 77
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> First Turn Process: The player going second channels an extra Rune from their Rune Deck during their first Channel Phase of the game.

### [official-rule] 484.4.a - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

Official rule text:

> Each player provides three (3) Battlefields, included in their deck during deck building.

- section_id: 484

### [official-rule] 484.5.a - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.5
- section_id: 484
- page: 78
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Each player who is not going first randomly selects one (1) of their three (3) Battlefields. The other two are removed and will not be used for this game. The selected Battlefields are placed simultaneously between the players before play and will be used for this game.

### [official-rule] 484.5.b - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.5
- section_id: 484

- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The player who is taking the first turn removes their Battlefields. They will not be used.

### [official-rule] 484.5.c - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.5
- section_id: 484
- page: 78
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Turn order alternates teams.

### [official-rule] 484.5.c.1 - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.5.c
- section_id: 484
- page: 78
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The first player will be followed by an opponent, then the first player's teammate, then the first opponent's teammate, and so on.

### [official-rule] 484.5.c.2 - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.5.c
- section_id: 484
- page: 78
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If allies are sitting across from each other, turn order proceeds clockwise as normal.

### [official-rule] 484.5.c.3 - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.5.c
- section_id: 484
- page: 78
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If allies are sitting next to each other, turn order is passed across the table.

### [official-rule] 484.6 - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484
- section_id: 484
- page: 78
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Format: Best of 1. The first team to reach the Victory Score in Points wins the Match.

### [official-rule] 484.6.a - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.6
- section_id: 484
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Teammates win or lose together.

### [official-rule] 484.6.a.1 - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.6.a
- section_id: 484
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If one player Concedes, then the entire team loses.

### [official-rule] 484.6.a.2 - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.6.a
- section_id: 484
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If one player Loses, then the entire team loses.

### [official-rule] 484.6.a.3 - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.6.a
- section_id: 484
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If one player Wins, then the entire team wins.

### [official-rule] 484.7 - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484
- section_id: 484
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> First Turn Process: The player going first does not draw a card during their first Draw Phase of the game. The player going last channels an extra Rune from their Rune Deck during their first Channel Phase of the game.

### [official-rule] 484.8 - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484
- section_id: 484
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Unique Rules

### [official-rule] 484.8.a - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.8
- section_id: 484
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Players may play spells or activate abilities during their Teammate's Turn. In order to do so, their Teammate will invite them to play a spell or activate an ability using their own Priority.

### [official-rule] 484.8.b - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.8
- section_id: 484
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Battlefields controlled during the Beginning Phase of a player's turn by that player's teammate are disqualified from being scored by that Team, that turn. Example: A player may not induce their partner to retreat, and then conquer a Battlefield their teammate was controlling.

### [official-rule] 484.8.c - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.8
- section_id: 484
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Control is not shared.

### [official-rule] 484.8.c.1 - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.8.c
- section_id: 484
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Players may not Hide cards at Battlefields controlled by their Teammate.

### [official-rule] 484.8.c.2 - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.8.c
- section_id: 484
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Players may not issue Standard Movement to their Teammate's Units.

### [official-rule] 484.8.d - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.8
- section_id: 484
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Friendly describes controlled Game Objects by a player or their Teammate. Example: "When I am played, ready a friendly unit" could target a player's own Units or their teammate's Units.

### [official-rule] 484.8.e - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.8
- section_id: 484
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Hands are Private Information. Players may show their Private Information to one or more players at any time during play, including their teammate.

### [official-rule] 484.8.e.1 - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.8.e
- section_id: 484
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Revealing Cards is still a game action and cannot be done unless instructed. See rule 424. Reveal for more information.

### [official-rule] 484.8.f - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.8
- section_id: 484
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The Final Point has an adjustment to the criteria when scoring.

### [official-rule] 484.8.f.1 - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.8.f
- section_id: 484
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When scoring the Final Point from Conquer, a player must Score each Battlefield in the same turn, excepting any that were occupied by their ally during that turn’s Beginning Phase.

### [official-rule] 484.8.f.2 - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.8.f
- section_id: 484
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> No other changes to Final Point restrictions.

### [official-rule] 484.8.g - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.8
- section_id: 484
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Teammates may not utilize the same Champion Legend.

### [official-rule] 484.8.h - 2v2 (Magma Chamber)

- parent_rule: 484.8
- section_id: 484
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

> Teammates may not utilize the same Battlefields.

### [official-rule] 649 - Conceding

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 649
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Conceding

### [official-rule] 650 - A player may concede at any time.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 650
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A player may concede at any time.

### [official-rule] 651 - When a player concedes, they are removed from the game in progress.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 651
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When a player concedes, they are removed from the game in progress.

### [official-rule] 651.1 - If only one other player is remaining after a player has conceded, the player remaining...

- parent_rule: 651
- section_id: 651
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If only one other player is remaining after a player has conceded, the player remaining Wins.

### [official-rule] 651.2 - If more than one player remains after a concession, follow the steps for the Removal of...

- parent_rule: 651
- section_id: 651
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If more than one player remains after a concession, follow the steps for the Removal of a Player.

### [official-rule] 651.3 - Removal of a player involves them no longer being able to make choices or otherwise inf...

- parent_rule: 651
- section_id: 651
- page: 79
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Removal of a player involves them no longer being able to make choices or otherwise influence the game.

### [official-rule] 651.4 - If the player has Teammates due to the Mode of Play, that player’s Teammates also lose...

- parent_rule: 651
- section_id: 651
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the player has Teammates due to the Mode of Play, that player’s Teammates also lose and are removed from the game.

### [official-rule] 652 - If the game continues, follow these steps for Removal of a Player.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 652
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the game continues, follow these steps for Removal of a Player.

### [official-rule] 652.1 - Banish all permanents, runes, and facedown cards they currently control and all permane...

- parent_rule: 652
- section_id: 652
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Banish all permanents, runes, and facedown cards they currently control and all permanents, runes, and facedown cards they own.

### [official-rule] 652.2 - Remove the Battlefield they contributed to the game if it is in use.

- parent_rule: 652
- section_id: 652
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Remove the Battlefield they contributed to the game if it is in use.

### [official-rule] 652.2.a - If it was in use, Replace it with a token battlefield with no abilities.

- parent_rule: 652.2
- section_id: 652
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If it was in use, Replace it with a token battlefield with no abilities.

### [official-rule] 652.2.b - Any units or hidden cards there do not move and are otherwise unaffected by this proces...

- parent_rule: 652.2
- section_id: 652
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Any units or hidden cards there do not move and are otherwise unaffected by this process. See rule 438. Replace for more information.

### [official-rule] 652.2.c - If the removed battlefield was applying any continuous effects, those continuous effect...

- parent_rule: 652.2
- section_id: 652
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the removed battlefield was applying any continuous effects, those continuous effects immediately cease, which may cause changes in the characteristics of units or hidden cards there. Example: A battlefield reads "Units here have +1 [M]." If that battlefield's owner concedes and the battlefield is removed from the game as a result, units there immediately cease to get +1 [M].

### [official-rule] 652.3 - Remove all cards they own from the game.

- parent_rule: 652
- section_id: 652
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Remove all cards they own from the game.

### [official-rule] 652.4 - Counter all spells and abilities of all types controlled by the player that has conceded.

- parent_rule: 652
- section_id: 652
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Counter all spells and abilities of all types controlled by the player that has conceded.

### [official-rule] 652.5 - Proceed with the game.

- parent_rule: 652
- section_id: 652
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Proceed with the game.

### [official-rule] 652.5.a - Turn

- parent_rule: 652.5
- section_id: 652
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Turn

### [official-rule] 652.5.a.1 - If the removed player was the Turn Player, play proceeds in Turn Order to the next avai...

- parent_rule: 652.5.a
- section_id: 652
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the removed player was the Turn Player, play proceeds in Turn Order to the next available player in order.

### [official-rule] 652.5.b - Focus

- parent_rule: 652.5
- section_id: 652
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Focus

### [official-rule] 652.5.b.1 - If the removed player had Focus in a Showdown, the next Player in order receives Focus.

- parent_rule: 652.5.b
- section_id: 652
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the removed player had Focus in a Showdown, the next Player in order receives Focus.

### [official-rule] 652.5.b.2 - If the player being removed creates a state where all Players have Passed their Focus,...

- parent_rule: 652.5.b
- section_id: 652
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the player being removed creates a state where all Players have Passed their Focus, the Showdown ends and play proceeds as necessary, E.G. Combat is resolved or a Cleanup is completed.

### [official-rule] 652.5.c - Priority

- parent_rule: 652.5
- section_id: 652
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Priority

### [official-rule] 652.5.c.1 - If the removed player had Priority during a Chain, the next Player in order receives Pr...

- parent_rule: 652.5.c
- section_id: 652
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the removed player had Priority during a Chain, the next Player in order receives Priority.

### [official-rule] 652.5.c.2 - If the player being removed creates a state where all Players have passed Priority, the...

- parent_rule: 652.5.c
- section_id: 652
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the player being removed creates a state where all Players have passed Priority, then the most recent spell or ability on the Chain will resolve with Priority being re-established as appropriate for the state after the resolution of that spell or ability.

### [official-rule] 700 - Additional Rules

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 700
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Additional Rules

### [official-rule] 701 - Buffs

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 701
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Buffs

### [official-rule] 702 - Buffs are objects placed on Units.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 702
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Buffs are objects placed on Units.

### [official-rule] 702.1 - Buffs can be tracked with a buff reminder card from a Riftbound booster pack or with an...

- parent_rule: 702
- section_id: 702
- page: 80
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Buffs can be tracked with a buff reminder card from a Riftbound booster pack or with any spare object in your surroundings.

### [official-rule] 702.2 - Buffs can be added or spent.

- parent_rule: 702
- section_id: 702
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Buffs can be added or spent.

### [official-rule] 702.2.a - To Buff a Unit, a player chooses a Unit and then places a buff on it. That Unit is Buff...

- parent_rule: 702.2
- section_id: 702
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> To Buff a Unit, a player chooses a Unit and then places a buff on it. That Unit is Buffed for as long as the buff remains on it.

### [official-rule] 702.2.b - Spending a Buff removes a single Buff counter from a Unit.

- parent_rule: 702.2
- section_id: 702
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Spending a Buff removes a single Buff counter from a Unit.

### [official-rule] 702.2.b.1 - A buff cannot be spent from a Unit that does not have a buff.

- parent_rule: 702.2.b
- section_id: 702
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A buff cannot be spent from a Unit that does not have a buff.

### [official-rule] 702.2.b.2 - A player can only spend buffs on units they control.

- parent_rule: 702.2.b
- section_id: 702
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A player can only spend buffs on units they control.

### [official-rule] 702.3 - There can only be one Buff on a Unit at a time.

- parent_rule: 702
- section_id: 702
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> There can only be one Buff on a Unit at a time.

### [official-rule] 702.3.a - If a Buff is added, or instructed to be added, on a Unit that already has a Buff, it is...

- parent_rule: 702.3
- section_id: 702
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If a Buff is added, or instructed to be added, on a Unit that already has a Buff, it is not placed instead.

### [official-rule] 703 - Each Buff individually contributes +1 Might to a Unit.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 703
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Each Buff individually contributes +1 Might to a Unit.

### [official-rule] 704 - Buffs are Game Objects and may be referenced, counted, or targeted by other effects as...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 704
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Buffs are Game Objects and may be referenced, counted, or targeted by other effects as specified.

### [official-rule] 705 - If a Unit leaves play, remove all Buffs from it.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 705
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If a Unit leaves play, remove all Buffs from it.

### [official-rule] 705.1 - Champions do not retain Buffs in the Champion Zone, even if they return there somehow.

- parent_rule: 705
- section_id: 705
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Champions do not retain Buffs in the Champion Zone, even if they return there somehow.

### [official-rule] 706 - Mighty

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 706
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Mighty

### [official-rule] 707 - Mighty is a description that applies to some units. Other game effects can check whethe...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 707
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Mighty is a description that applies to some units. Other game effects can check whether a unit is Mighty.

### [official-rule] 708 - A Unit "is Mighty" as long as its Might is 5 or greater.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 708
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Unit "is Mighty" as long as its Might is 5 or greater.

### [official-rule] 709 - A Unit "becomes Mighty" at the moment its Might changes from being less than 5 to being...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 709
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Unit "becomes Mighty" at the moment its Might changes from being less than 5 to being 5 or greater. Example: A Unit with Might 4 that gets +1 [M] becomes Mighty. Example: A Unit with Might 5 that gets +1 [M] does not become Mighty, because it was already Mighty.

### [official-rule] 710 - Units on the board are evaluated according to their current Might. Example: A unit with...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 710
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Units on the board are evaluated according to their current Might. Example: A unit with a base Might of 3 is targeted by a spell that reads "A unit gets +3 [M] this turn." As that spell resolves, its Might changes from 3 to 6, and it becomes Mighty. When that effect expires at the end of the turn, it will no longer be Mighty.

### [official-rule] 711 - Units in Non-Board Zones are evaluated according to their inherent Might. Example: A un...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 711
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Units in Non-Board Zones are evaluated according to their inherent Might. Example: A unit in the trash is Mighty if its printed Might is 5 or greater. It doesn't matter if there were effects raising or lowering its might while it was on the board.

### [official-rule] 712 - Bonus Damage

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 712
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Bonus Damage

### [official-rule] 713 - Bonus Damage is an intrinsic property that can be granted to Deal actions that influenc...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 713
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Bonus Damage is an intrinsic property that can be granted to Deal actions that influence the amount of Damage that the action is distributing.

### [official-rule] 714 - If more than one instance of Bonus Damage is applied or granted to a Deal action, all i...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 714
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If more than one instance of Bonus Damage is applied or granted to a Deal action, all instances are summed and applied once.

### [official-rule] 714.1 - Bonus Damage can only be a positive value, and can only increase the amount of Damage b...

- parent_rule: 714
- section_id: 714
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Bonus Damage can only be a positive value, and can only increase the amount of Damage being distributed.

### [official-rule] 714.2 - If, for any reason, Bonus Damage would be a negative number, then no Bonus Damage is ap...

- parent_rule: 714
- section_id: 714
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If, for any reason, Bonus Damage would be a negative number, then no Bonus Damage is applied to the action.

### [official-rule] 715 - Bonus Damage applies to the total damage Dealt by one instance of the action.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 715
- page: 81
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Bonus Damage applies to the total damage Dealt by one instance of the action.

### [official-rule] 715.1 - If the Deal action has a single target, the amount of Damage to that target will be inc...

- parent_rule: 715
- section_id: 715
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the Deal action has a single target, the amount of Damage to that target will be increased by the Bonus Damage granted to it.

### [official-rule] 715.2 - If the Deal action has multiple targets, the amount of Damage dealt to each target is i...

- parent_rule: 715
- section_id: 715
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the Deal action has multiple targets, the amount of Damage dealt to each target is increased by Bonus Damage individually and separately. Example: Singularity is a spell that says “Deal 6 to each of up to two units.” A player plays Singularity while they also control Annie, Fiery, a unit that says “Your spells and abilities deal 1 Bonus Damage.” Singularity deals 1 Bonus Damage to both of its targets, dealing 7 to each.

### [official-rule] 715.3 - If the Deal action Splits damage, then the Bonus Damage applies to the amount of Damage...

- parent_rule: 715
- section_id: 715
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the Deal action Splits damage, then the Bonus Damage applies to the amount of Damage that will be Split. This can alter the number of targets eligible to be chosen. Example: Volibear, Furious is a unit that says in part “When I attack, deal 5 damage split among any number of enemy units here.” A player attacks with Volibear, Furious while they also control Annie, Fiery, a unit that says “Your spells and abilities deal 1 Bonus Damage.” Volibear, Furious now deals 6 damage split among any number of enemy units at its location, and can choose to split that damage among up to 6 units rather than the usual 5.

### [official-rule] 715.4 - If no damage was Dealt, because it was replaced or reduced by any means, then Bonus Dam...

- parent_rule: 715
- section_id: 715
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If no damage was Dealt, because it was replaced or reduced by any means, then Bonus Damage will not apply.

### [official-rule] 716 - Attachment

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 716
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Attachment

### [official-rule] 717 - Attaching is a limited action that causes cards to become linked to each other to combi...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 717
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Attaching is a limited action that causes cards to become linked to each other to combine their effects in some way. This causes one card to become Attached and the other to become A Top-Most Card. See rule 434. Attach for more information.

### [official-rule] 718 - Attached is the state of a card being linked to another card in this way.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 718
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Attached is the state of a card being linked to another card in this way.

### [official-rule] 718.1 - A card remains in this state until Detached.

- parent_rule: 718
- section_id: 718
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A card remains in this state until Detached.

### [official-rule] 718.2 - While in this state, the card’s printed Rules Text is Inactive. See rule 720. Inactive...

- parent_rule: 718
- section_id: 718
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> While in this state, the card’s printed Rules Text is Inactive. See rule 720. Inactive for more information.

### [official-rule] 718.3 - While in this state, Abilities in the card’s Effect Text are appended to the Rules Text...

- parent_rule: 718
- section_id: 718
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> While in this state, Abilities in the card’s Effect Text are appended to the Rules Text of the Top-Most Card.

### [official-rule] 718.4 - While in this state, the card’s Might Bonus modulates the Top-Most Card’s Might by the...

- parent_rule: 718
- section_id: 718
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> While in this state, the card’s Might Bonus modulates the Top-Most Card’s Might by the value listed.

### [official-rule] 718.5 - Attached cards still have all properties of being a card on the board while in this state.

- parent_rule: 718
- section_id: 718
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Attached cards still have all properties of being a card on the board while in this state.

### [official-rule] 718.5.a - Attached cards still have all Types and Tags while Attached.

- parent_rule: 718.5
- section_id: 718
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Attached cards still have all Types and Tags while Attached.

### [official-rule] 718.5.b - Attached cards still can be chosen or targeted by game effects while Attached.

- parent_rule: 718.5
- section_id: 718
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Attached cards still can be chosen or targeted by game effects while Attached.

### [official-rule] 718.5.c - Attached cards cannot be moved separately from the Top-Most Card they are Attached to.

- parent_rule: 718.5
- section_id: 718
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Attached cards cannot be moved separately from the Top-Most Card they are Attached to.

### [official-rule] 718.5.d - A card may be Attached only to a single Top-Most card at a time.

- parent_rule: 718.5
- section_id: 718
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A card may be Attached only to a single Top-Most card at a time.

### [official-rule] 718.5.e - Attached cards may have different Controllers from their Top-Most card.

- parent_rule: 718.5
- section_id: 718
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Attached cards may have different Controllers from their Top-Most card.

### [official-rule] 718.5.f - Changes in Control of the Top-Most card do not impact Control of Attached cards and vic...

- parent_rule: 718.5
- section_id: 718
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Changes in Control of the Top-Most card do not impact Control of Attached cards and vice versa.

### [official-rule] 718.5.g - An Attached card still appends the abilities in its Effect Text to the Rules Text of th...

- parent_rule: 718.5
- section_id: 718
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> An Attached card still appends the abilities in its Effect Text to the Rules Text of the Top-Most card and modulates the Top-Most Card’s Might by its Might Bonus.

### [official-rule] 719 - A Top-Most Card is a card that has one or more cards linked to it through the process o...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 719
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Top-Most Card is a card that has one or more cards linked to it through the process of Attaching.

### [official-rule] 719.1 - The Effect Text of all cards Attached to this card are appended to the Rules Text of th...

- parent_rule: 719
- section_id: 719
- page: 82
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The Effect Text of all cards Attached to this card are appended to the Rules Text of this card for as long as they remain Attached.

### [official-rule] 719.2 - This card ceases being a Top-Most Card when there are no longer any cards Attached to it.

- parent_rule: 719
- section_id: 719
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> This card ceases being a Top-Most Card when there are no longer any cards Attached to it.

### [official-rule] 719.3 - A Top-Most Card and all cards Attached to it are at the same location.

- parent_rule: 719
- section_id: 719
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Top-Most Card and all cards Attached to it are at the same location.

### [official-rule] 719.3.a - When the Top-Most Card moves, all Attached cards move with it.

- parent_rule: 719.3
- section_id: 719
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When the Top-Most Card moves, all Attached cards move with it.

### [official-rule] 719.4 - The Exhausted and Ready state of the Top-Most card does not affect nor change the statu...

- parent_rule: 719
- section_id: 719
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The Exhausted and Ready state of the Top-Most card does not affect nor change the status of the Attached cards and vice versa.

### [official-rule] 719.5 - When a Top-Most Card changes zones from a board zone to a non-board zone, all Attached...

- parent_rule: 719
- section_id: 719
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When a Top-Most Card changes zones from a board zone to a non-board zone, all Attached cards Detach from it, remaining in their current zones.

### [official-rule] 719.5.a - The player that controls the Top-Most Card that changed zones decides the order these c...

- parent_rule: 719.5
- section_id: 719
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The player that controls the Top-Most Card that changed zones decides the order these cards Detach in, and thus the order of any relevant effects that occur due to the Detach occurring.

### [official-rule] 720 - Inactive

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 720
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Inactive

### [official-rule] 721 - Card text can occasionally be assigned to be ignored, disregarded, or otherwise rendere...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 721
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Card text can occasionally be assigned to be ignored, disregarded, or otherwise rendered as not applicable during the course of play. This state is referred to as Inactive.

### [official-rule] 721.1 - Text marked this way is not applied at all while in this state.

- parent_rule: 721
- section_id: 721
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Text marked this way is not applied at all while in this state.

### [official-rule] 721.2 - Inactive Abilities do not trigger, do not apply, and cannot be activated. Inactive inst...

- parent_rule: 721
- section_id: 721
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Inactive Abilities do not trigger, do not apply, and cannot be activated. Inactive instructions are not processed.

### [official-rule] 722 - Inactive text is still present on cards.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 722
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Inactive text is still present on cards.

### [official-rule] 722.1 - Cards with Inactive text still have keywords for the sake of Game Effects that want to...

- parent_rule: 722
- section_id: 722
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Cards with Inactive text still have keywords for the sake of Game Effects that want to reference or see if a card has a keyword.

### [official-rule] 722.2 - Game Effects that parse or interpret text to determine target eligibility may still par...

- parent_rule: 722
- section_id: 722
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Game Effects that parse or interpret text to determine target eligibility may still parse Inactive text for the sake of eligibility. Example: Spinning Axe is a gear with [Temporary]. While it’s attached and its rules text is inactive, its [Temporary] ability doesn’t trigger. However, a spell that reads “Destroy a gear with [Temporary]” could still choose and destroy Spinning Axe.

### [official-rule] 723 - Rules Text is never Inactive by default.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 723
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Rules Text is never Inactive by default.

### [official-rule] 724 - Effect Text is Inactive unless the card with the Effect Text is Attached.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 724
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Effect Text is Inactive unless the card with the Effect Text is Attached.

### [official-rule] 725 - Inactive text can partially cease to be Inactive under specific circumstances and excep...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 725
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Inactive text can partially cease to be Inactive under specific circumstances and exceptions.

### [official-rule] 725.1 - If an Attached card has a Passive or Replacement ability that applies during the proces...

- parent_rule: 725
- section_id: 725
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If an Attached card has a Passive or Replacement ability that applies during the process of Attaching or a Triggered ability that triggers off of Attaching, that text exists and can be processed as it Attaches.

### [official-rule] 725.2 - If an Attached card has a Passive or Replacement ability that applies during the proces...

- parent_rule: 725
- section_id: 725
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If an Attached card has a Passive or Replacement ability that applies during the process of Detaching or a Triggered ability that triggers off of Detaching, that text exists and can be processed as it Detaches.

### [official-rule] 725.3 - If an Attached card has an Equip ability, the Weaponmaster keyword can reference that E...

- parent_rule: 725
- section_id: 725
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If an Attached card has an Equip ability, the Weaponmaster keyword can reference that Equip ability and any abilities that passively modify that Equip ability.

### [official-rule] 726 - Dependent Keywords

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 726
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Dependent Keywords

### [official-rule] 727 - Keywords can be Dependent Keywords

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 727
- page: 83
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Keywords can be Dependent Keywords

### [official-rule] 727.1 - Keywords can be Dependent Keywords

- parent_rule: 727
- section_id: 727
- page: 83-84
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Dependent Keyword is comprised of both a Condition that it is short for, and an ability of some format immediately after the Keyword itself. EXAMPLE: Noxus Hopeful has “[Legion][>] I cost [2] less.” [Legion] is short for the condition “if you have played another card this turn, this card gains [Text],” while “I cost [2] less” is the dependent ability.

### [official-rule] 727.1.a - Keywords can be Dependent Keywords

- parent_rule: 727.1
- section_id: 727
- page: 84
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A Dependent Keyword will always be functionally short for a Condition.

### [official-rule] 727.1.a.1 - Keywords can be Dependent Keywords

- parent_rule: 727.1.a
- section_id: 727
- page: 84
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> This Condition may also have a determined Duration, Time, or Limitation as part of its definition. EXAMPLE: Legion specifies the duration of its condition: “if you have played another card this turn.” This turn is the duration. EXAMPLE: A dependent keyword that is short for “Until you have spent [3] this turn” would include a limitation on its condition. EXAMPLE: A dependent keyword that is short for “After you’ve become an attacker this turn” would include a time after which the condition is active.

### [official-rule] 727.1.b - Keywords can be Dependent Keywords

- parent_rule: 727.1
- section_id: 727
- page: 84
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The Dependent Ability associated with the Dependent Keyword is Inactive on the card with the Dependent Keyword until the Condition is met, when it becomes Active

### [official-rule] 727.1.b.1 - Keywords can be Dependent Keywords

- parent_rule: 727.1.b
- section_id: 727
- page: 84
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The Dependent Ability is present on the card for the sake of reference or evaluation until the Condition is met See rule 720. Inactive for more information

### [official-rule] 727.1.b.2 - Keywords can be Dependent Keywords

- parent_rule: 727.1.b
- section_id: 727
- page: 84
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The Dependent Ability is Active exactly as written while the Condition is true Example: Gustwalker has “[Level3][>] I have +1 [M] and Ganking.” As long as its controller has 3 XP, Gustwalker’s Ganking is active.

### [official-rule] 727.1.c - Keywords can be Dependent Keywords

- parent_rule: 727.1
- section_id: 727
- page: 84
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The Dependent Abilities of Dependent Keywords can be of any type

### [official-rule] 727.1.c.1 - Keywords can be Dependent Keywords

- parent_rule: 727.1.c
- section_id: 727
- page: 84
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Triggered Abilities of Dependent Keywords must be Active for their trigger to be evaluated.

### [official-rule] 727.1.c.1.a - Keywords can be Dependent Keywords

- parent_rule: 727.1.c.1
- section_id: 727
- page: 84
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If a Triggered Ability becomes active at the same time as its trigger condition would be fulfilled, it triggers.

### [official-rule] 727.1.c.2 - Keywords can be Dependent Keywords

- parent_rule: 727.1.c
- section_id: 727
- page: 84
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Passive Abilities begin applying at the same time the Dependent Keyword becomes true.

### [official-rule] 727.1.c.3 - Keywords can be Dependent Keywords

- parent_rule: 727.1.c
- section_id: 727
- page: 84
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Activated Abilities that become Active from Dependent Keywords can be activated at their associated timing after that ability has been granted

### [official-rule] 727.1.c.3.a - Keywords can be Dependent Keywords

- parent_rule: 727.1.c.3
- section_id: 727
- page: 84
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If the condition for the Dependent Keyword causes the Activated Ability to become Inactive after it has been added to the chain as a Pending Item, that chain item will not be affected and will proceed with being played as normal.

### [official-rule] 728 - XP

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 728
- page: 84
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> XP

### [official-rule] 729 - XP is a resource that is accrued, spent, or otherwise modified by Players through the c...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 729
- page: 84
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> XP is a resource that is accrued, spent, or otherwise modified by Players through the course of play.

### [official-rule] 729.1 - The amount of XP that a player has should be marked clearly.

- parent_rule: 729
- section_id: 729
- page: 84
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The amount of XP that a player has should be marked clearly.

### [official-rule] 729.1.a - XP is a value that can be tracked by counters, dice, cards, or any method that clearly...

- parent_rule: 729.1
- section_id: 729
- page: 84
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> XP is a value that can be tracked by counters, dice, cards, or any method that clearly displays the specific value of XP that is currently on a Player.

### [official-rule] 729.2 - The amount of XP a Player has is Public Information.

- parent_rule: 729
- section_id: 729
- page: 84
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> The amount of XP a Player has is Public Information.

### [official-rule] 730 - XP can be Gained and Spent.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 730
- page: 84
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> XP can be Gained and Spent.

### [official-rule] 730.1 - To Gain XP, increase the value of XP marked on the Player gaining it.

- parent_rule: 730
- section_id: 730
- page: 84
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> To Gain XP, increase the value of XP marked on the Player gaining it.

### [official-rule] 730.2 - To Spend XP, reduce the value of XP marked on the Player spending it.

- parent_rule: 730
- section_id: 730
- page: 84
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> To Spend XP, reduce the value of XP marked on the Player spending it.

### [official-rule] 731 - XP is not a Game Object.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 731
- page: 85
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> XP is not a Game Object.

### [official-rule] 731.1 - XP cannot be targeted, readied, or exhausted.

- parent_rule: 731
- section_id: 731
- page: 85
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> XP cannot be targeted, readied, or exhausted.

### [official-rule] 732 - XP is not shared between Allies in Game Modes with Teammates.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 732
- page: 85
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> XP is not shared between Allies in Game Modes with Teammates.

### [official-rule] 733 - There is no limit to an amount of XP a player can accrue.

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 733
- page: 85
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> There is no limit to an amount of XP a player can accrue.

### [official-rule] 734 - Additional Turns

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 734
- page: 85
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Additional Turns

### [official-rule] 735 - Certain Game Effects will instruct a player to “take a turn after this.” These effects...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 735
- page: 85
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Certain Game Effects will instruct a player to “take a turn after this.” These effects create a temporary Additional Turn owned by that player that is inserted into the turn queue after the current turn.

### [official-rule] 736 - Turn order is established when the game begins as a repeating set of players. This popu...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 736
- page: 85
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Turn order is established when the game begins as a repeating set of players. This populates a looping queue of turns that each player will take, starting with the first turn taken by the First Player, and repeating indefinitely.

### [official-rule] 737 - When an Additional Turn is inserted into this queue, it does not change the Turn Order...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 737
- page: 85
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> When an Additional Turn is inserted into this queue, it does not change the Turn Order of the game. The owner of the Additional Turn just has the next queued turn. After that turn is completed, it will be removed and the queue will proceed with its previously queued turns.

### [official-rule] 738 - If multiple Additional Turns are queued, they are added to the queue in the order the G...

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 738
- page: 85
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> If multiple Additional Turns are queued, they are added to the queue in the order the Game Effects that generated them occurred. Example: The First Player plays, through some means, two Time Warps during their turn. The Time Warps create two Additional Turns for their controller and insert them into the turn queue after the current turn. If the turn queue is represented as [> A > B > C > D >], then these Additional Turns will appear as [> A > A* > A* > B > C > D >]. After the last Additional Turn is played, the queue returns to its previously queued turns. The “*” denotes that a turn is an Additional Turn. Example: The First Player plays Promising Future during their turn, during the resolution of which the Second and Fourth Player choose, banish, and play one Time Warp each. The Fourth Player’s Time Warp resolves first, inserting an Additional Turn for them in the queue as such: [> A > D* > B > C > D >]. The Second Player’s Time Warp resolves afterwards, inserting that turn: [> A > B* > D* > B > C > D >]. When the First Player passes the turn, the Second Player will take their turn, followed by the Fourth Player, after which the queue returns to its previously queued turns.

### [official-rule] 739 - Special Terms

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 739
- page: 85
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Special Terms

### [official-rule] 740 - Some card text refers to Game Objects in particular ways:

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 740
- page: 85
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Some card text refers to Game Objects in particular ways:

### [official-rule] 740.1 - Some card text refers to Game Objects in particular ways:

- parent_rule: 740
- section_id: 740
- page: 85
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Two Game Objects are friendly if they share a controller, or if one’s controller is teammates with the other’s.

### [official-rule] 740.2 - Some card text refers to Game Objects in particular ways:

- parent_rule: 740
- section_id: 740
- page: 85
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Two Game Objects are enemies if one’s controller is an opponent of the other’s.

### [official-rule] 741 - Some card text refers to Units in particular ways:

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 741
- page: 85
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Some card text refers to Units in particular ways:

### [official-rule] 741.1 - Some card text refers to Units in particular ways:

- parent_rule: 741
- section_id: 741
- page: 85
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A unit is alone when there are no other friendly units at the same location.

### [official-rule] 741.2 - Some card text refers to Units in particular ways:

- parent_rule: 741
- section_id: 741
- page: 85
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A unit is one on one when it and the enemy unit at the same location are both alone.

### [official-rule] 741.3 - Some card text refers to Units in particular ways:

- parent_rule: 741
- section_id: 741
- page: 85
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> A unit is in combat if it is occupying a battlefield where combat is ongoing and has a combat designation.

### [official-rule] 742 - Some terms in this document are used in particular ways:

- parent_rule: None
- section_id: 742
- page: 85
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Some terms in this document are used in particular ways:

### [official-rule] 742.1 - Some terms in this document are used in particular ways:

- parent_rule: 742
- section_id: 742
- page: 85
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:

> Costs within instructions are instructions that take a particular form (“[do X] to [do Y]”). [Do X] is the cost associated with the instruction, and [do Y] is the effect.

### [official-rule] 742.1.a - Some terms in this document are used in particular ways:

- parent_rule: 742.1
- section_id: 742
- page: 85
- source: core-rules-20260330.pdf

Official rule text:
